// Pure source-coordinate math. No Unity/GPU device, cache singleton or RGB data.
public static class SourceAnchorMath37 {
 public static double[] World(double[][] triangle,double[] bary,double[] localToWorld){
  if(triangle==null||triangle.Length!=3||bary==null||bary.Length!=3||localToWorld==null||localToWorld.Length!=16)throw new ArgumentException("source anchor shape");
  double sum=bary[0]+bary[1]+bary[2];if(Math.Abs(sum-1)>1e-8||Array.Exists(bary,v=>Double.IsNaN(v)||Double.IsInfinity(v)||v<0||v>1))throw new ArgumentException("source barycentric domain");
  if(Array.Exists(localToWorld,v=>Double.IsNaN(v)||Double.IsInfinity(v))||localToWorld[3]!=0||localToWorld[7]!=0||localToWorld[11]!=0||localToWorld[15]!=1)throw new ArgumentException("affine source transform");
  var p=new double[3];for(int c=0;c<3;c++){if(triangle[c]==null||triangle[c].Length!=3||Array.Exists(triangle[c],v=>Double.IsNaN(v)||Double.IsInfinity(v)))throw new ArgumentException("source triangle");for(int k=0;k<3;k++)p[k]+=bary[c]*triangle[c][k];}
  var w=new double[3];for(int k=0;k<3;k++)w[k]=localToWorld[k]*p[0]+localToWorld[4+k]*p[1]+localToWorld[8+k]*p[2]+localToWorld[12+k];return w;
 }
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public static class Contour40 {
 public sealed class Result {public Vector3[] vertices;public Vector2[] provenance;public int[] indices,sourceVertexIndices;public int openEdges,nonmanifoldEdges,degenerateTriangles,sourceEdges,closedChains,openChains,branchVertices;}
 sealed class Edge {public int a,b;public List<int> triangles=new List<int>();}
 sealed class Segment {public int id;public Edge edge;}
 public static Result Build(Vector3[] local,int[] tri,int[] weld,Matrix4x4 transform,Func<Vector3,Vector2> project){
  if(local==null||tri==null||weld==null||local.Length!=weld.Length||tri.Length%3!=0||project==null)throw new ArgumentException("40 source topology ABI");
  var world=local.Select(transform.MultiplyPoint3x4).ToArray();var projected=world.Select(project).ToArray();var representatives=new Dictionary<int,Vector3>();
  for(int i=0;i<weld.Length;i++){if(!float.IsFinite(world[i].x)||!float.IsFinite(world[i].y)||!float.IsFinite(world[i].z)||!float.IsFinite(projected[i].x)||!float.IsFinite(projected[i].y))throw new ArgumentException("40 finite evaluated source required");if(representatives.TryGetValue(weld[i],out var old)){if(!old.Equals(world[i]))throw new InvalidOperationException("40 registered equivalent source vertices deformed differently");}else representatives.Add(weld[i],world[i]);}
  var sign=new int[tri.Length/3];var edges=new Dictionary<ulong,Edge>();var r=new Result();
  for(int t=0;t<sign.Length;t++){
   int a=tri[t*3],b=tri[t*3+1],c=tri[t*3+2];if(a<0||b<0||c<0||a>=local.Length||b>=local.Length||c>=local.Length)throw new ArgumentException("40 source index out of range");
   var x=projected[a];var y=projected[b];var z=projected[c];double det=((double)y.x-x.x)*((double)z.y-x.y)-((double)y.y-x.y)*((double)z.x-x.x);sign[t]=Math.Sign(det);if(sign[t]==0)r.degenerateTriangles++;
   foreach(var p in new[]{new[]{a,b},new[]{b,c},new[]{c,a}}){uint lo=(uint)Math.Min(weld[p[0]],weld[p[1]]),hi=(uint)Math.Max(weld[p[0]],weld[p[1]]);if(lo==hi)continue;ulong key=((ulong)lo<<32)|hi;if(!edges.TryGetValue(key,out var e)){e=new Edge{a=p[0],b=p[1]};edges.Add(key,e);}e.triangles.Add(t);}
  }
  var v=new List<Vector3>();var uv=new List<Vector2>();var indices=new List<int>();var sourceVertices=new List<int>();int id=0;var segments=new List<Segment>();
  foreach(var pair in edges.OrderBy(p=>p.Key)){id++;var e=pair.Value;if(e.triangles.Count==1){r.openEdges++;continue;}if(e.triangles.Count!=2){r.nonmanifoldEdges++;continue;}int a=sign[e.triangles[0]],b=sign[e.triangles[1]];if(a!=b)segments.Add(new Segment{id=id,edge=e});}
  var nodes=new Dictionary<int,List<int>>();for(int i=0;i<segments.Count;i++){var e=segments[i].edge;foreach(int node in new[]{weld[e.a],weld[e.b]}){if(!nodes.TryGetValue(node,out var list)){list=new List<int>();nodes.Add(node,list);}list.Add(i);}}
  r.branchVertices=nodes.Count(p=>p.Value.Count>2);var used=new bool[segments.Count];
  // D3D aliased lines use diamond exit. Orient each actual source chain
  // coherently so both incident segments never terminate at a degree2 joint.
  // This changes neither the source edge set nor depth/occlusion testing.
  void Walk(int first,int initial){int current=first,node=initial;while(!used[current]){used[current]=true;var s=segments[current];var e=s.edge;int a=weld[e.a]==node?e.a:e.b,b=a==e.a?e.b:e.a;int start=v.Count;v.Add(world[a]);v.Add(world[b]);sourceVertices.Add(a);sourceVertices.Add(b);uv.Add(new Vector2(s.id,weld[a]<weld[b]?0:1));uv.Add(new Vector2(s.id,weld[b]<weld[a]?0:1));indices.Add(start);indices.Add(start+1);node=weld[b];if(nodes[node].Count!=2)break;int next=nodes[node][0]==current?nodes[node][1]:nodes[node][0];if(used[next])break;current=next;}if(node==initial)r.closedChains++;else r.openChains++;}
  foreach(var p in nodes.Where(p=>p.Value.Count!=2).OrderBy(p=>p.Key))foreach(int i in p.Value)if(!used[i])Walk(i,p.Key);
  for(int i=0;i<segments.Count;i++)if(!used[i])Walk(i,Math.Min(weld[segments[i].edge.a],weld[segments[i].edge.b]));
  r.vertices=v.ToArray();r.provenance=uv.ToArray();r.indices=indices.ToArray();r.sourceVertexIndices=sourceVertices.ToArray();r.sourceEdges=edges.Count;return r;
 }
}
}


namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public class Json40 : IEnumerable<Json40>
{
    protected Dictionary<string,Json40> fields;protected List<Json40> items;protected object value;
    public Json40 this[string key] {get {Json40 v;return fields!=null&&fields.TryGetValue(key,out v)?v:null;}set {fields[key]=value;}}
    public Json40 this[int i] {get {return items[i];}}
    public int Count {get {return items!=null?items.Count:fields!=null?fields.Count:0;}}
    public void Add(Json40 v) {items.Add(v);}
    public IEnumerable<T> Values<T>() {return this.Select(v=>(T)Convert.ChangeType(v.value,typeof(T),CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));}
    public IEnumerator<Json40> GetEnumerator() {return (items??(fields==null?new List<Json40>():fields.Values.ToList())).GetEnumerator();}
    IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator() {return GetEnumerator();}
    public static implicit operator Json40(string v) {return v==null?null:new Json40{value=v};}
    public static implicit operator Json40(int v) {return new Json40{value=v};}
    public static implicit operator Json40(long v) {return new Json40{value=v};}
    public static implicit operator Json40(float v) {return new Json40{value=(double)v};}
    public static implicit operator Json40(double v) {return new Json40{value=v};}
    public static implicit operator Json40(bool v) {return new Json40{value=v};}
    public static explicit operator string(Json40 v) {return v==null?null:Convert.ToString(v.value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
    public static explicit operator int(Json40 v) {return Convert.ToInt32(v.value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
    public static explicit operator long(Json40 v) {return Convert.ToInt64(v.value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
    public static explicit operator double(Json40 v) {return Convert.ToDouble(v.value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
    public static explicit operator bool(Json40 v) {return Convert.ToBoolean(v.value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
    public static Json40 Wrap(object v) {
        if(v==null)return null;if(v is Json40)return(Json40)v;if(v is string)return(string)v;if(v is bool)return(bool)v;
        if(v is double)return(double)v;if(v is float)return(float)v;if(v is int)return(int)v;if(v is long)return(long)v;
        if(v is IEnumerable){var a=new Arr40();foreach(var x in(IEnumerable)v)a.Add(Wrap(x));return a;}
        throw new ArgumentException("Unsupported JSON boundary value: "+v.GetType().Name);
    }
    static string Quote(string text) {
        var b=new StringBuilder("\"");foreach(char c in text){switch(c){case '"':b.Append("\\\"");break;case '\\':b.Append("\\\\");break;case '\b':b.Append("\\b");break;case '\f':b.Append("\\f");break;case '\n':b.Append("\\n");break;case '\r':b.Append("\\r");break;case '\t':b.Append("\\t");break;default:if(c<32)b.Append("\\u"+((int)c).ToString("x4"));else b.Append(c);break;}}return b.Append('"').ToString();
    }
    public override string ToString() {
        if(fields!=null)return "{"+string.Join(",",fields.OrderBy(p=>p.Key,StringComparer.Ordinal).Select(p=>Quote(p.Key)+":"+(p.Value==null?"null":p.Value.ToString())))+"}";
        if(items!=null)return "["+string.Join(",",items.Select(v=>v==null?"null":v.ToString()))+"]";
        if(value==null)return "null";if(value is string)return Quote((string)value);if(value is bool)return(bool)value?"true":"false";
        if(value is double){double x=(double)value;if(double.IsNaN(x)||double.IsInfinity(x))throw new ArgumentException("Nonfinite JSON number");return x.ToString("R",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);}
        return Convert.ToString(value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }
    public static bool DeepEquals(Json40 a,Json40 b) {return a==null?b==null:b!=null&&a.ToString()==b.ToString();}
    public const int MaxInputChars66=65536;
    public static Json40 Parse(string text) {if(text.Length>MaxInputChars66)throw new ArgumentException("Small JSON budget exceeded; use streaming verification");int p=0;var result=ParseValue(text,ref p,0);Space(text,ref p);if(p!=text.Length)throw new ArgumentException("Trailing JSON data");return result;}
    static void Space(string s,ref int p) {while(p<s.Length&&char.IsWhiteSpace(s[p]))p++;}
    static string ParseString(string s,ref int p) {
        if(s[p++]!='"')throw new ArgumentException("JSON string expected");var b=new StringBuilder();
        while(p<s.Length){char c=s[p++];if(c=='"')return b.ToString();if(c=='\\'){char e=s[p++];switch(e){case '"':case '\\':case '/':b.Append(e);break;case 'b':b.Append('\b');break;case 'f':b.Append('\f');break;case 'n':b.Append('\n');break;case 'r':b.Append('\r');break;case 't':b.Append('\t');break;case 'u':b.Append((char)int.Parse(s.Substring(p,4),NumberStyles.HexNumber,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));p+=4;break;default:throw new ArgumentException("JSON escape");}}else{if(c<32)throw new ArgumentException("JSON control character");b.Append(c);}}
        throw new ArgumentException("Unterminated JSON string");
    }
    static Json40 ParseValue(string s,ref int p,int depth) {
        if(depth>128)throw new ArgumentException("JSON nesting limit");Space(s,ref p);if(p>=s.Length)throw new ArgumentException("JSON value expected");char c=s[p];
        if(c=='"')return ParseString(s,ref p);
        if(c=='{'||c=='['){bool obj=c=='{';p++;Json40 result=obj?(Json40)new Obj40():new Arr40();Space(s,ref p);if(p<s.Length&&s[p]==(obj?'}':']')){p++;return result;}
            while(true){Space(s,ref p);string key=null;if(obj){key=ParseString(s,ref p);if(result.fields.ContainsKey(key))throw new ArgumentException("Duplicate JSON member");Space(s,ref p);if(s[p++]!=':')throw new ArgumentException("JSON colon");}var v=ParseValue(s,ref p,depth+1);if(obj)result[key]=v;else result.Add(v);Space(s,ref p);char end=s[p++];if(end==(obj?'}':']'))break;if(end!=',')throw new ArgumentException("JSON separator");}return result;}
        foreach(string word in new[]{"true","false","null"})if(p+word.Length<=s.Length&&string.CompareOrdinal(s,p,word,0,word.Length)==0){p+=word.Length;return word=="null"?null:(Json40)(word=="true");}
        int start=p;while(p<s.Length&&"-+0123456789.eE".IndexOf(s[p])>=0)p++;if(start==p)throw new ArgumentException("JSON token");return double.Parse(s.Substring(start,p-start),CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }
}
public sealed class Obj40 : Json40 {public Obj40(){fields=new Dictionary<string,Json40>(StringComparer.Ordinal);}public new static Obj40 Parse(string text){return(Obj40)Json40.Parse(text);}}
public sealed class Arr40 : Json40 {public Arr40(params object[] values){items=new List<Json40>();if(values.Length==1&&values[0] is IEnumerable&&!(values[0] is string)&&!(values[0] is Json40)){foreach(var v in(IEnumerable)values[0])Add(Wrap(v));}else foreach(var v in values)Add(Wrap(v));}}

// Offline66 candidate: no Unity/API/thread/process/GC actions.

public static class SerializedContent40
{
 public const int SmallSemanticBudgetBytes=65536;
 public const int CharChunk=8192,ByteChunk=32768;
 public sealed class Comparison
 {
  public string Status,BeforeSHA256,AfterSHA256,Reason;
  public long BeforeBytes,AfterBytes,HashInputBytes;
  public bool? Equivalent;
 }
 static string Finish(SHA256 h)
 {
  h.TransformFinalBlock(new byte[0],0,0);
  return BitConverter.ToString(h.Hash).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();
 }
 static string HashFile(string path,out long length)
 {
  length=0;
  using(var h=SHA256.Create())using(var f=File.OpenRead(path))
  {
   var buffer=new byte[ByteChunk];int n;
   while((n=f.Read(buffer,0,buffer.Length))>0){h.TransformBlock(buffer,0,n,buffer,0);length+=n;}
   return Finish(h);
  }
 }
 static string HashText(string text,out long length)
 {
  length=0;var chars=new char[CharChunk];var bytes=new byte[ByteChunk];
  var encoder=new UTF8Encoding(false,true).GetEncoder();
  using(var h=SHA256.Create())
  {
   int position=0;bool completed=false;
   do
   {
    int count=Math.Min(chars.Length,text.Length-position);
    text.CopyTo(position,chars,0,count);
    int charsUsed,bytesUsed;
    encoder.Convert(chars,0,count,bytes,0,bytes.Length,position+count==text.Length,out charsUsed,out bytesUsed,out completed);
    if(charsUsed==0&&bytesUsed==0&&!completed)throw new InvalidOperationException("UTF8 encoder made no progress");
    position+=charsUsed;
    if(bytesUsed>0){h.TransformBlock(bytes,0,bytesUsed,bytes,0);length+=bytesUsed;}
   }while(position<text.Length||!completed);
   return Finish(h);
  }
 }
 static bool RequiresExactNumericReview(string text)
 {
  int i=0;
  while(i<text.Length)
  {
   if(text[i]=='"')
   {i++;while(i<text.Length){char c=text[i++];if(c=='\\'){if(i<text.Length)i++;}else if(c=='"')break;}continue;}
   char first=text[i];
   if(first=='-'||(first>='0'&&first<='9'))
   {
    int j=i;if(text[j]=='-')j++;int significant=0;bool started=false;
    while(j<text.Length)
    {
     char c=text[j];if(c=='.'){j++;continue;}
     if(c<'0'||c>'9')break;if(c!='0')started=true;if(started)significant++;j++;
    }
    if(significant>15)return true;
    if(j<text.Length&&(text[j]=='e'||text[j]=='E')){j++;if(j<text.Length&&(text[j]=='+'||text[j]=='-'))j++;while(j<text.Length&&text[j]>='0'&&text[j]<='9')j++;}
    i=Math.Max(i+1,j);continue;
   }
   i++;
  }
  return false;
 }
 public static Comparison Verify(string beforePath,string currentSerialized)
 {
  if(currentSerialized==null)throw new ArgumentNullException("currentSerialized");
  var r=new Comparison();long before,after;
  r.BeforeSHA256=HashFile(beforePath,out before);r.AfterSHA256=HashText(currentSerialized,out after);
  r.BeforeBytes=before;r.AfterBytes=after;r.HashInputBytes=before+after;
  if(before==after&&r.BeforeSHA256==r.AfterSHA256)
  {r.Status="ExactUTF8";r.Equivalent=true;r.Reason="Same serialized UTF8 fingerprint; no JSON parse/re-serialization.";return r;}
  if(before>SmallSemanticBudgetBytes||after>SmallSemanticBudgetBytes)
  {r.Status="UnverifiedRepresentationChange";r.Equivalent=null;r.Reason="Large nonidentical input exceeds bounded semantic fallback; not declared changed or equivalent.";return r;}
  string previous=File.ReadAllText(beforePath,new UTF8Encoding(false,true));
  if(RequiresExactNumericReview(previous)||RequiresExactNumericReview(currentSerialized))
  {r.Status="UnverifiedNumericPrecision";r.Equivalent=null;r.Reason="No double-rounding equivalence for >15 significant digits.";return r;}
  try
  {
   r.Equivalent=Json40.DeepEquals(Json40.Parse(previous),Json40.Parse(currentSerialized));
   r.Status=r.Equivalent.Value?"SmallSemanticEquivalent":"SmallSemanticDifferent";
   r.Reason="Fallback <=64KiB; no suffix copying. High-precision numbers/duplicate keys are not accepted as equivalent.";
  }
  catch(Exception e)
  {
   if(!(e is ArgumentException)&&!(e is FormatException)&&!(e is OverflowException)&&!(e is IndexOutOfRangeException))throw;
   r.Status="UnverifiedInvalidOrUnsupportedJSON";r.Equivalent=null;r.Reason=e.GetType().Name+": "+e.Message;
  }
  return r;
 }
}

public static class CreateOnly40
{
 public static void Text(string path,string text)
 {using(var f=new FileStream(path,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read))using(var w=new StreamWriter(f,new UTF8Encoding(false))){w.Write(text);w.Flush();f.Flush(true);}}
}

public static class NativeSnapshot40
{
 public static Arr40 Backup(string root)
 {
  var all=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<UnityEngine.Object>();var rows=new Arr40();bool player=false;int i=0;
  foreach(var o in all.Where(x=>x&&EditorUtility.IsPersistent(x)).OrderBy(x=>AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(x)))
  {
   string path=AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(o);if(!path.StartsWith("Assets/")&&!path.StartsWith("ProjectSettings/"))continue;
   if(!EditorUtility.IsDirty(o)&&!path.StartsWith("ProjectSettings/"))continue;
   bool dirty=EditorUtility.IsDirty(o);string name="native-before-"+(i++)+".json";string file=Path.Combine(root,name);if(File.Exists(file))throw new IOException("Snapshot already exists");
   string text=EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(o);CreateOnly40.Text(file,text);var c=SerializedContent40.Verify(file,text);if(c.Equivalent!=true)throw new IOException("Native backup failed: "+path);
   rows.Add(new Obj40{["path"]=path,["type"]=o.GetType().FullName,["entityId"]=o.GetEntityId().ToString(),["snapshot"]=name,["sha256"]=c.BeforeSHA256,["bytes"]=c.BeforeBytes,["dirtyBefore"]=dirty,["dirtyAfter"]=EditorUtility.IsDirty(o)});
   if(o is PlayerSettings)player=true;
  }
  if(!player)throw new InvalidOperationException("Full loaded PlayerSettings snapshot unavailable; no Play");
  CreateOnly40.Text(Path.Combine(root,"NATIVE_BEFORE73.json"),rows.ToString());return rows;
 }
 public static Arr40 Compare(string root,Arr40 rows)
 {
  var all=Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<UnityEngine.Object>();var checks=new Arr40();
  foreach(Obj40 row in rows)
  {
   string path=(string)row["path"],type=(string)row["type"];var o=all.FirstOrDefault(x=>x&&AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(x)==path&&x.GetType().FullName==type);
   if(!o){checks.Add(new Obj40{["path"]=path,["type"]=type,["status"]="Unavailable",["equal"]=null});continue;}
   var c=SerializedContent40.Verify(Path.Combine(root,(string)row["snapshot"]),EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(o));checks.Add(new Obj40{["path"]=path,["type"]=type,["status"]=c.Status,["equal"]=c.Equivalent.HasValue?(Json40)c.Equivalent.Value:null,["beforeSHA"]=c.BeforeSHA256,["afterSHA"]=c.AfterSHA256,["dirty"]=EditorUtility.IsDirty(o)});
  }
  return checks;
 }
}

// Prepared only; not imported, compiled in Unity, or executed in task64.
// Public journal + externally hashed binary backup, never private-instance-only.

public sealed class GlobalJournal40
{
    public enum Kind { Float,LegacyInt,Integer,Vector,Matrix,Texture,FloatArray,VectorArray,MatrixArray,Buffer,ConstantBuffer }
    public sealed class Spec {public string name;public Kind kind;}
    public sealed class Entry
    {
        public Spec spec;public float scalar;public int integer;public Vector4 vector;public Matrix4x4 matrix;
        public float[] floats;public Vector4[] vectors;public Matrix4x4[] matrices;
        public Texture originalTexture;public string textureKind,assetGUID;public long localId;
        public bool restorable,mutated;public string reason;
    }
    public static GlobalJournal40 Active;
    public readonly List<Entry> entries=new List<Entry>();
    public string sourceId,externalPath,backupSHA256;
    public bool externalVerified;

    public static GlobalJournal40 Snapshot(string sourceId,string path,IEnumerable<Spec> specs)
    {
        if(Active!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("existing journal requires inspection, not replacement");
        var j=new GlobalJournal40{sourceId=sourceId,externalPath=path};
        foreach(var s in specs)
        {
            if(j.entries.Any(e=>e.spec.name==s.name))throw new ArgumentException("duplicate global name");
            var e=new Entry{spec=s,restorable=true};
            switch(s.kind)
            {
                case Kind.Float:e.scalar=Shader.GetGlobalFloat(s.name);break;
                // Legacy SetGlobalInt uses float storage in Unity6000.5. Keep
                // the full original float, not only its integer truncation.
                case Kind.LegacyInt:e.scalar=Shader.GetGlobalFloat(s.name);e.integer=Shader.GetGlobalInt(s.name);break;
                case Kind.Integer:e.integer=Shader.GetGlobalInteger(s.name);break;
                case Kind.Vector:e.vector=Shader.GetGlobalVector(s.name);break;
                case Kind.Matrix:e.matrix=Shader.GetGlobalMatrix(s.name);break;
                case Kind.FloatArray:e.floats=Shader.GetGlobalFloatArray(s.name);e.restorable=e.floats!=null&&e.floats.Length>0;break;
                case Kind.VectorArray:e.vectors=Shader.GetGlobalVectorArray(s.name);e.restorable=e.vectors!=null&&e.vectors.Length>0;break;
                case Kind.MatrixArray:e.matrices=Shader.GetGlobalMatrixArray(s.name);e.restorable=e.matrices!=null&&e.matrices.Length>0;break;
                case Kind.Texture:
                    e.originalTexture=Shader.GetGlobalTexture(s.name);
                    if(!e.originalTexture)e.textureKind="null";
                    else if(e.originalTexture==Texture2D.blackTexture)e.textureKind="builtin-black";
                    else if(e.originalTexture==Texture2D.whiteTexture)e.textureKind="builtin-white";
                    else if(e.originalTexture==Texture2D.grayTexture)e.textureKind="builtin-gray";
                    else {e.restorable=EditorUtility.IsPersistent(e.originalTexture)&&AssetDatabase.TryGetGUIDAndLocalFileIdentifier(e.originalTexture,out e.assetGUID,out e.localId);e.textureKind=e.restorable?"persistent-asset":"runtime-unrecoverable";}
                    break;
                case Kind.Buffer:case Kind.ConstantBuffer:e.restorable=false;e.reason="No public Shader global binding getter; do not mutate";break;
            }
            if(!e.restorable&&string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.reason))e.reason="absent/zero array or runtime-only texture; do not mutate";
            j.entries.Add(e);
        }
        j.WriteExternal();Active=j;return j;
    }

    static void Matrix(BinaryWriter w,Matrix4x4 m){for(int c=0;c<4;c++)for(int r=0;r<4;r++)w.Write(m[r,c]);}
    static void Vector(BinaryWriter w,Vector4 v){w.Write(v.x);w.Write(v.y);w.Write(v.z);w.Write(v.w);}
    void WriteExternal()
    {
        using(var stream=new FileStream(externalPath,FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read))
        using(var w=new BinaryWriter(stream))
        {
            w.Write(0x34364a47);w.Write(1);w.Write(sourceId);w.Write(entries.Count);
            foreach(var e in entries)
            {
                w.Write(e.spec.name);w.Write((int)e.spec.kind);w.Write(e.restorable);w.Write(e.reason??"");
                w.Write(e.scalar);w.Write(e.integer);Vector(w,e.vector);Matrix(w,e.matrix);
                w.Write(e.textureKind??"");w.Write(e.assetGUID??"");w.Write(e.localId);
                w.Write(e.floats?.Length??0);if(e.floats!=null)foreach(var v in e.floats)w.Write(v);
                w.Write(e.vectors?.Length??0);if(e.vectors!=null)foreach(var v in e.vectors)Vector(w,v);
                w.Write(e.matrices?.Length??0);if(e.matrices!=null)foreach(var v in e.matrices)Matrix(w,v);
            }
            w.Flush();stream.Flush(true);
        }
        using(var h=SHA256.Create())using(var f=File.OpenRead(externalPath))backupSHA256=BitConverter.ToString(h.ComputeHash(f)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();
        externalVerified=VerifyExternal();
        if(!externalVerified)throw new IOException("external backup verification failed");
    }

    static bool Bits(float a,float b){return BitConverter.SingleToInt32Bits(a)==BitConverter.SingleToInt32Bits(b);}
    static bool VectorBits(Vector4 a,Vector4 b){for(int i=0;i<4;i++)if(!Bits(a[i],b[i]))return false;return true;}
    static bool MatrixBits(Matrix4x4 a,Matrix4x4 b){for(int i=0;i<16;i++)if(!Bits(a[i],b[i]))return false;return true;}
    static bool ReadVector(BinaryReader r,Vector4 expected){for(int i=0;i<4;i++)if(!Bits(r.ReadSingle(),expected[i]))return false;return true;}
    static bool ReadMatrix(BinaryReader r,Matrix4x4 expected){for(int c=0;c<4;c++)for(int row=0;row<4;row++)if(!Bits(r.ReadSingle(),expected[row,c]))return false;return true;}
    bool VerifyExternal()
    {
        using(var r=new BinaryReader(File.OpenRead(externalPath)))
        {
            if(r.ReadInt32()!=0x34364a47||r.ReadInt32()!=1||r.ReadString()!=sourceId||r.ReadInt32()!=entries.Count)return false;
            foreach(var e in entries)
            {
                if(r.ReadString()!=e.spec.name||r.ReadInt32()!=(int)e.spec.kind||r.ReadBoolean()!=e.restorable||r.ReadString()!=(e.reason??""))return false;
                if(!Bits(r.ReadSingle(),e.scalar)||r.ReadInt32()!=e.integer||!ReadVector(r,e.vector)||!ReadMatrix(r,e.matrix))return false;
                if(r.ReadString()!=(e.textureKind??"")||r.ReadString()!=(e.assetGUID??"")||r.ReadInt64()!=e.localId)return false;
                if(r.ReadInt32()!=(e.floats?.Length??0))return false;if(e.floats!=null)foreach(float v in e.floats)if(!Bits(r.ReadSingle(),v))return false;
                if(r.ReadInt32()!=(e.vectors?.Length??0))return false;if(e.vectors!=null)foreach(var v in e.vectors)if(!ReadVector(r,v))return false;
                if(r.ReadInt32()!=(e.matrices?.Length??0))return false;if(e.matrices!=null)foreach(var v in e.matrices)if(!ReadMatrix(r,v))return false;
            }
            return r.BaseStream.Position==r.BaseStream.Length;
        }
    }

    // Call on the ENTIRE renderer mutation inventory before normal Play/render.
    // This does not intercept or excuse untracked setters inside product passes.
    public void RequireRecoverable(IEnumerable<string> mutationNames)
    {
        if(!externalVerified)throw new InvalidOperationException("external backup not verified");
        foreach(string name in mutationNames)
        {var e=entries.SingleOrDefault(x=>x.spec.name==name);if(e==null||!e.restorable)throw new InvalidOperationException("unbacked global mutation: "+name);}
    }
    public void BeforeMutation(string name,int newArrayLength=-1)
    {
        RequireRecoverable(new[]{name});var e=entries.Single(x=>x.spec.name==name);
        int old=e.floats?.Length??e.vectors?.Length??e.matrices?.Length??-1;
        if(newArrayLength>=0&&newArrayLength!=old)throw new InvalidOperationException("global array length change forbidden");
        e.mutated=true;
    }
    public void RestoreMarked()
    {
        // No buffer setters. Assets and built-ins are retained references, never
        // destroyed/recreated from a guessed EntityId or surrounding colours.
        foreach(var e in entries.Where(x=>x.mutated))
        {
            if(!e.restorable)throw new InvalidOperationException("unrestorable global: "+e.spec.name);
            string n=e.spec.name;
            switch(e.spec.kind)
            {
                case Kind.Float:Shader.SetGlobalFloat(n,e.scalar);break;
                case Kind.LegacyInt:Shader.SetGlobalFloat(n,e.scalar);break;
                case Kind.Integer:Shader.SetGlobalInteger(n,e.integer);break;
                case Kind.Vector:Shader.SetGlobalVector(n,e.vector);break;
                case Kind.Matrix:Shader.SetGlobalMatrix(n,e.matrix);break;
                case Kind.Texture:
                    if(e.textureKind!="null"&&!e.originalTexture)throw new InvalidOperationException("original texture lifetime lost");
                    Shader.SetGlobalTexture(n,e.originalTexture);break;
                case Kind.FloatArray:Shader.SetGlobalFloatArray(n,e.floats);break;
                case Kind.VectorArray:Shader.SetGlobalVectorArray(n,e.vectors);break;
                case Kind.MatrixArray:Shader.SetGlobalMatrixArray(n,e.matrices);break;
                default:throw new InvalidOperationException("unsupported restore type");
            }
        }
        // Getter-by-getter equality verification is a required caller step before
        // clearing Active; this helper does not clear the only recovery handle.
    }
    public string[] VerifyMarked()
    {
        var errors=new List<string>();
        foreach(var e in entries.Where(x=>x.mutated))
        {
            string n=e.spec.name;bool same=false;
            switch(e.spec.kind)
            {
                case Kind.Float:case Kind.LegacyInt:same=Bits(Shader.GetGlobalFloat(n),e.scalar);break;
                case Kind.Integer:same=Shader.GetGlobalInteger(n)==e.integer;break;
                case Kind.Vector:same=VectorBits(Shader.GetGlobalVector(n),e.vector);break;
                case Kind.Matrix:same=MatrixBits(Shader.GetGlobalMatrix(n),e.matrix);break;
                case Kind.Texture:same=Shader.GetGlobalTexture(n)==e.originalTexture;break;
                case Kind.FloatArray:var a=Shader.GetGlobalFloatArray(n);same=a!=null&&a.Length==e.floats.Length&&a.Zip(e.floats,Bits).All(v=>v);break;
                case Kind.VectorArray:var b=Shader.GetGlobalVectorArray(n);same=b!=null&&b.Length==e.vectors.Length&&b.Zip(e.vectors,VectorBits).All(v=>v);break;
                case Kind.MatrixArray:var c=Shader.GetGlobalMatrixArray(n);same=c!=null&&c.Length==e.matrices.Length&&c.Zip(e.matrices,MatrixBits).All(v=>v);break;
            }
            if(!same)errors.Add(n);
        }
        return errors.ToArray();
    }
    public void ReleaseHandleAfterVerified()
    {
        if(!externalVerified||VerifyMarked().Length!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("restore not verified; preserve recovery handle");
        // Drop only journal references, never Dispose/Destroy original textures
        // or user buffers. No forced GC. External backup stays on disk.
        entries.Clear();if(Active==this)Active=null;
    }
}

// CPU-only contracts shared by the reviewed candidate. No Unity dependency.

public enum GridSpace40 { PhysicalHead, CanonicalAuthored }
public sealed class GridBinding40 {
 public readonly string sourceId,viewId,modelKey;
 public readonly GridSpace40 space;
 public readonly int frame,sequence,x,y,width,height,scale,pixelWidth,pixelHeight;
 readonly float[] clip;
 public GridBinding40(string sourceId,string viewId,string modelKey,GridSpace40 space,int frame,int sequence,
  int x,int y,int width,int height,int scale,int pixelWidth,int pixelHeight,float[] clip){
  this.sourceId=sourceId;this.viewId=viewId;this.modelKey=modelKey;this.space=space;this.frame=frame;this.sequence=sequence;
  this.x=x;this.y=y;this.width=width;this.height=height;this.scale=scale;this.pixelWidth=pixelWidth;this.pixelHeight=pixelHeight;
  this.clip=clip==null?null:(float[])clip.Clone();
 }
 public float[] CopyClip(){return clip==null?null:(float[])clip.Clone();}
 public double HeadX(int u){if(u<0||u>=pixelWidth)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();return x+(u+.5)/scale;}
 public double HeadY(int v){if(v<0||v>=pixelHeight)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();return y+height-(v+.5)/scale;}
 static void Need(bool ok,string why){if(!ok)throw new ArgumentException(why);}
 static void Validate(GridBinding40 g){
  Need(g!=null,"grid missing");Need(g.space==GridSpace40.PhysicalHead,"canonical input is not a physical source grid");
  Need(!String.IsNullOrEmpty(g.sourceId)&&!String.IsNullOrEmpty(g.viewId)&&!String.IsNullOrEmpty(g.modelKey),"source/view/model identity missing");
  Need(g.frame>=0&&g.sequence>0,"frame/sequence missing");
  Need(g.x>=0&&g.y>=0&&g.width>0&&g.height>0&&(long)g.x+g.width<=480&&(long)g.y+g.height<=272,"head ROI outside fixed480x272 contract");
  Need(g.scale==8&&(long)g.width*g.height<=4096,"physical8x ROI budget");
  Need(g.pixelWidth==(long)g.width*g.scale&&g.pixelHeight==(long)g.height*g.scale&&(long)g.pixelWidth*g.pixelHeight<=262144,"raster dimensions do not match sample lattice");
  Need(g.clip!=null&&g.clip.Length==16,"world-to-cropped-clip matrix missing");
  foreach(float v in g.clip)Need(!Single.IsNaN(v)&&!Single.IsInfinity(v),"nonfinite projection");
 }
 public static void RequirePair(GridBinding40 expected,GridBinding40 outline,GridBinding40 pigment){
  Validate(expected);Validate(outline);Validate(pigment);
  foreach(var g in new[]{outline,pigment}){
   Need(g.sourceId==expected.sourceId&&g.viewId==expected.viewId&&g.modelKey==expected.modelKey&&g.frame==expected.frame&&g.sequence==expected.sequence,"frame/view/source generation mismatch");
   Need(g.x==expected.x&&g.y==expected.y&&g.width==expected.width&&g.height==expected.height&&g.scale==expected.scale,"physical sample origin/ROI mismatch");
   for(int i=0;i<16;i++)Need(g.clip[i]==expected.clip[i],"producer projection differs from shared frame");
  }
 }
}


// Pure managed policy used by the unactivated diagnostic candidate.
public sealed class Submission40 {
 readonly object sync=new object();readonly string key;readonly int generation;
 int stages;bool commands,barrierAttempted,callbackSeen,complete,failed,closed;
 public Submission40(string key,int generation){if(String.IsNullOrEmpty(key)||generation<=0)throw new ArgumentException("submission identity");this.key=key;this.generation=generation;}
 public void BeforeCommands(){lock(sync){if(closed||barrierAttempted)throw new InvalidOperationException("submission sealed");commands=true;}}
 public void StageRecorded(int stage){lock(sync){
  if(!commands||failed||closed||barrierAttempted)throw new InvalidOperationException("no active submission");
  if(stage!=1&&stage!=2&&stage!=4&&stage!=8)throw new ArgumentException("stage");
  if((stages&stage)!=0||(stage==8&&stages!=7))throw new InvalidOperationException("duplicate or unordered producer");stages|=stage;
 }}
 public void Fail(){lock(sync){failed=true;}}
 // Set before invoking the API. An exception here is uncertain, never a retry.
 public void BeforeBarrier(){lock(sync){if(barrierAttempted)throw new InvalidOperationException("end barrier already attempted");barrierAttempted=true;}}
 public void AcceptCallback(string ownerKey,int ownerGeneration,bool done,bool error){lock(sync){
  if(ownerKey!=key||ownerGeneration!=generation){failed=true;return;}
  if(!barrierAttempted||!commands){failed=true;return;}
  if(callbackSeen){failed=true;return;} // Never turn a later callback into a retry.
  if(!done){failed=true;return;}callbackSeen=true;
  if(error){failed=true;return;}complete=true;
 }}
 public void Stop(){lock(sync){closed=true;}}
 public bool CanRelease{get{lock(sync){return !commands||complete;}}}
 public bool CanUsePixels{get{lock(sync){return complete&&!failed&&stages==15;}}}
 public bool HasError{get{lock(sync){return failed;}}}
 public bool CommandsPossible{get{lock(sync){return commands;}}}
}

// Stop/request-exit, owned-resource drain, and Edit restoration have separate
// idempotent progress. Pending GPU work never prevents the one normal-exit call
// or causes a timeout/retry/forced resource release.
public sealed class ExitCoordinator40 {
 readonly List<string> errors=new List<string>();bool begun,releaseAttempted,restoreAttempted,completionBroken;
 public bool ExitAttempted{get;private set;}public bool Released{get;private set;}public bool Restored{get;private set;}
 public string[] Errors=>errors.ToArray();
 void Attempt(string stage,Action action){try{action();}catch(Exception e){errors.Add(stage+": "+e.GetType().Name+": "+e.Message);}}
 public void Begin(Action stop,Action restoreBeforeExit,Action requestNormalExit){
  if(begun)return;begun=true;
  Attempt("stop producers",stop);Attempt("restore before exit",restoreBeforeExit);
  ExitAttempted=true;Attempt("normal exit",requestNormalExit);
 }
 public void Pump(bool stableEdit,Func<bool> mayRelease,Action releaseOwned,Action restoreAfterEdit){
  if(!begun)return;
  if(!releaseAttempted&&!completionBroken){bool allowed=false;try{allowed=mayRelease();}catch(Exception e){completionBroken=true;errors.Add("completion check: "+e.GetType().Name+": "+e.Message);}
   if(allowed){releaseAttempted=true;Attempt("release owned",()=>{releaseOwned();Released=true;});}}
  if(stableEdit&&!restoreAttempted){restoreAttempted=true;Attempt("restore after Edit",()=>{restoreAfterEdit();Restored=true;});}
 }
 public bool Finished=>begun&&Released&&Restored;
}

// Small strong-root table for pending owners, not a resource manager. One owner
// per live view; another view and another owner cannot release its slot.
public sealed class PendingViews40<T> where T:class {
 readonly Dictionary<string,T> slots=new Dictionary<string,T>();
 public void Acquire(string view,T owner){if(String.IsNullOrEmpty(view)||owner==null)throw new ArgumentException("view owner");if(slots.ContainsKey(view))throw new InvalidOperationException("view has unsettled owner");slots.Add(view,owner);}
 public void Release(string view,T owner){if(!slots.TryGetValue(view,out var current)||!System.Object.ReferenceEquals(current,owner))throw new InvalidOperationException("foreign owner cannot release view");slots.Remove(view);}
 public bool Owns(string view,T owner)=>slots.TryGetValue(view,out var current)&&System.Object.ReferenceEquals(current,owner);
 public int Count=>slots.Count;
}

public static class QMetaLattice40 {
 public static int[] RasterOrigin(int[]qROI,bool startsAtTop){return new[]{qROI[0],startsAtTop?qROI[1]:272-qROI[1]-qROI[3]};}
 public static void Require(int[] sourceROI,int[] qROI,int qWidth,int qHeight,int metaWidth,int metaHeight){
  if(sourceROI==null||qROI==null||sourceROI.Length!=4||qROI.Length!=4)throw new ArgumentException("ROI");
  int x=qROI[0],y=qROI[1],w=qROI[2],h=qROI[3];
  if(x<0||y<0||w<=0||h<=0||(long)x+w>480||(long)y+h>272||qWidth!=w||qHeight!=h||metaWidth!=w||metaHeight!=h)throw new ArgumentException("same-fragment logical grid required");
  if(sourceROI[0]<x||sourceROI[1]<y||sourceROI[2]<=0||sourceROI[3]<=0||(long)sourceROI[0]+sourceROI[2]>(long)x+w||(long)sourceROI[1]+sourceROI[3]>(long)y+h)throw new ArgumentException("source outside Q/meta domain");
 }
 public static int[] MapBottomLeft(int headX,int headYBottom,int[]qROI){
  int x=headX-qROI[0],y=headYBottom-(272-qROI[1]-qROI[3]);
  if(x<0||y<0||x>=qROI[2]||y>=qROI[3])throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("Q/meta sample outside paired domain");return new[]{x,y};
 }
 public static bool Known(float level,float qDepth,float qAlpha,float metaDepth,float metaAlpha){
  foreach(float v in new[]{level,qDepth,qAlpha,metaDepth,metaAlpha})if(Single.IsNaN(v)||Single.IsInfinity(v))return false;
  return qDepth>0&&qAlpha>0&&qDepth==metaDepth&&qAlpha==metaAlpha;
 }
}

// Pure managed lifetime for later diagnostic consumers of producer-owned RTs.
public sealed class ConsumerReads40 {
 public sealed class Ticket{internal Ticket(){}}
 readonly object sync=new object();Ticket active;int bytes;bool closed,failed,rejected;
 public Ticket Begin(int expectedBytes){lock(sync){if(closed||failed||active!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("32 closed/failed/pending consumer; no duplicate submission");if(expectedBytes<=0||expectedBytes>262144)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(expectedBytes));bytes=expectedBytes;active=new Ticket();return active;}}
 public void Complete(Ticket token,bool done,bool error,int actualBytes){lock(sync){
  if(token==null||!System.Object.ReferenceEquals(token,active)){failed=true;return;}
  if(rejected){failed=true;return;}
  if(!done||error||actualBytes!=bytes){failed=true;rejected=true;return;}
  active=null;bytes=0;
 }}
 public void Fail(){lock(sync){failed=true;}}
 public void Seal(){lock(sync){closed=true;}}
 public bool CanRelease{get{lock(sync){return active==null;}}}
 public bool HasError{get{lock(sync){return failed;}}}
 public static int ColourBytes(int x,int y,int width,int height,int targetWidth,int targetHeight){
  if(x<0||y<0||width<=0||height<=0||targetWidth<=0||targetHeight<=0||(long)x+width>targetWidth||(long)y+height>targetHeight)throw new ArgumentException("32 readback ROI");
  long pixels=(long)width*height;if(pixels>262144/16)throw new ArgumentException("32 one bounded row/strip at a time");return checked((int)pixels*16);
 }
}

// Readback callbacks may request exit without touching any Editor API. Only the
// creating Editor thread can drain the request in its explicit observer tick.
public sealed class EditorExitRequest40 {
 readonly int ownerThread=Environment.CurrentManagedThreadId;int queued;bool handled;

 public void RequireOwner(){if(Environment.CurrentManagedThreadId!=ownerThread)throw new InvalidOperationException("32 drain on the creating Editor thread only");}
 public void Drain(Action begin){RequireOwner();if(!handled&&System.Threading.Volatile.Read(ref queued)!=0){handled=true;begin();}}
 public bool Requested=>System.Threading.Volatile.Read(ref queued)!=0;
}

  return crop*physical;
 }

 sealed class MaterialVersion31 {
  readonly Material material;readonly int crc;readonly Texture[] textures;readonly uint[] updates;readonly Hash128[] imageHashes;
  public MaterialVersion31(Material m){material=m;crc=m.ComputeCRC();textures=m.GetTexturePropertyNames().Select(m.GetTexture).ToArray();updates=textures.Select(t=>t?t.updateCount:0).ToArray();imageHashes=textures.Select(t=>t?t.imageContentsHash:default).ToArray();}
  public void Require(){if(!material||material.ComputeCRC()!=crc)throw new InvalidOperationException("31 source material generation changed");var now=material.GetTexturePropertyNames().Select(material.GetTexture).ToArray();if(!now.SequenceEqual(textures))throw new InvalidOperationException("31 source texture identity changed");for(int i=0;i<textures.Length;i++)if(textures[i]&&(textures[i].updateCount!=updates[i]||textures[i].imageContentsHash!=imageHashes[i]))throw new InvalidOperationException("31 source texture contents changed");}
 }

 void PrepareAnchor37(UnityEngine.Mesh mesh,Vector3[] vertices,Matrix4x4 transform){
  if(renderer!=(int)anchorDoc37["rendererOrdinal"]||submesh!=(int)anchorDoc37["subMesh"])throw new InvalidOperationException("37 carrier binding mismatch");
  string Hash(byte[] b){using(var h=SHA256.Create())return BitConverter.ToString(h.ComputeHash(b)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();}
  var indices=mesh.GetIndices(submesh);var ib=new byte[indices.Length*4];Buffer.BlockCopy(indices,0,ib,0,ib.Length);if(Hash(ib)!=(string)anchorDoc37["indexSHA256"])throw new InvalidOperationException("37 source indices invalidated");
  var uv=mesh.uv;var ub=new byte[uv.Length*8];using(var stream=new MemoryStream(ub))using(var writer=new BinaryWriter(stream)){foreach(var a in uv){writer.Write(a.x);writer.Write(a.y);}}if(Hash(ub)!=(string)anchorDoc37["meshUVSHA256"])throw new InvalidOperationException("37 source UV invalidated");
  int t=(int)anchorDoc37["triangleZeroBased"];var corners=anchorDoc37["corners"].Values<int>().ToArray();var b=anchorDoc37["barycentric"].Values<double>().ToArray();if(t<0||3*t+2>=indices.Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("37 source primitive unavailable");var local=new double[3][];var witness=Vector2.zero;
  for(int j=0;j<3;j++){int ix=indices[3*t+j];if(ix!=corners[j]||ix<0||ix>=vertices.Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("37 source primitive changed");var v=vertices[ix];local[j]=new double[]{v.x,v.y,v.z};witness+=uv[ix]*(float)b[j];}
  var expected=anchorDoc37["UV"].Values<double>().ToArray();if(Math.Abs(witness.x-expected[0])>2e-7||Math.Abs(witness.y-expected[1])>2e-7)throw new InvalidOperationException("37 barycentric UV mismatch");
  var world=SourceAnchorMath37.World(local,b,Enumerable.Range(0,16).Select(i=>(double)transform[i]).ToArray());var point=new Vector3((float)world[0],(float)world[1],(float)world[2]);var head=eye.full.Project(point);if(!float.IsFinite(head.x)||!float.IsFinite(head.y))throw new InvalidOperationException("37 source projection unavailable");Anchor37=new Obj40{["sourceKey"]=(string)anchorDoc37["sourceKey"],["actorInstance"]=eye.GetEntityId().ToString(),["camera"]=cameraIdentity,["triangle"]=t,["UV"]=anchorDoc37["UV"],["barycentric"]=anchorDoc37["barycentric"],["world"]=new Arr40(world),["head"]=new Arr40(head.x,head.y),["headPixel"]=new Arr40((int)Math.Floor(head.x),(int)Math.Floor(head.y)),["expectedDepth"]=-(camera.worldToCameraMatrix*new Vector4(point.x,point.y,point.z,1)).z,["indicesUVVerified"]=true};

 public override void RecordRenderGraph(RenderGraph graph,ContextContainer context){
  if(stopped||Disposed||recorded)return;if(Time.frameCount<TargetFrame40)return;if(Time.frameCount>TargetFrame40){Fail(new InvalidOperationException("Missed requested consecutive source frame; no late substitute"));return;}
  try{
   if(context.Get<UniversalCameraData>().camera!=camera)return;Prepare();submission=new Submission40(FrameKey,generation);recorded=true;
   // Conservatively retain resources even if graph recording aborts before execution.
   submission.BeforeCommands();
   foreach(int stage in new[]{1,2,4}){using var b=graph.AddUnsafePass<Data>("31 source producer "+stage,out var d);d.owner=this;d.stage=stage;
    if(stage==4){foreach(var t in QFields)b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(qDepth),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(fineDepth),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseAllGlobalTextures(true);var res=context.Get<UniversalResourceData>();if(res.mainShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(res.mainShadowsTexture);if(res.additionalShadowsTexture.IsValid())b.UseTexture(res.additionalShadowsTexture);}
    else{int start=stage==1?0:4;for(int i=start;i<start+4;i++)b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(Fields[i]),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(depth),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(styleDepth39),AccessFlags.Write);if(stage==1)foreach(var t61 in Fields.Skip(18).Take(8))b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(t61),AccessFlags.Write);int st39=stage==1?8:10;b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(Fields[st39]),AccessFlags.Write);b.UseTexture(graph.ImportTexture(Fields[st39+1]),AccessFlags.Write);}
    b.AllowPassCulling(false);b.SetRenderFunc(static(Data p,UnsafeGraphContext c)=>p.owner.Produce(c,p.stage));
   }

  // Deliberately runs even when a producer failed or exit has been requested.
  try{var ticket=submission;string key=FrameKey;int gen=generation;ticket.BeforeBarrier();var cmd=CommandBufferHelpers.GetNativeCommandBuffer(context.cmd);

  }catch(Exception e){Fail(e);}
 }

 // No raw owned RT escapes this variant. Copy at most262144B into managed data
 // during the callback; borrowed NativeArray/request lifetime cannot escape.
 public Vector2Int FieldExtent40(bool q,int index){RequireCompleted();var fields=q?QFields:Fields;if(index<0||index>=fields.Length)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(index));return new Vector2Int(fields[index].rt.width,fields[index].rt.height);}
 public void RequestFieldReadback40(bool qField,int index,RectInt rect,Action<byte[]> receive){
  var fields=qField?QFields:Fields;if(index<0||index>=fields.Length)throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(index));RequestOwned32(fields[index],rect,receive);
 }
 public void RequestClassificationReadback40(RectInt rect,Action<byte[]> receive){RequestOwned32(Classification,rect,receive);}
 void RequestOwned32(RTHandle target,RectInt rect,Action<byte[]> receive){
  RequireCompleted();if(receive==null)throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(receive));
  int bytes=ReadbackPreflight40.RequireColourSource(target.rt,rect.x,rect.y,rect.width,rect.height);var ticket=consumers32.Begin(bytes);
  try{AsyncGPUReadback.Request(target.rt,0,rect.x,rect.width,rect.y,rect.height,0,1,TextureFormat.RGBAFloat,r=>{
   bool done=false,error=true;int actual=0;
   try{done=r.done;error=r.hasError;if(done&&!error)actual=r.layerDataSize;
    if(!done||error||actual!=bytes)throw new InvalidOperationException("32 consumer readback incomplete/error/byte mismatch; retain resource");
    var data=r.GetData<byte>();if(data.Length!=bytes){actual=-1;throw new InvalidOperationException("32 unexpected payload length");}
    var copy=new byte[bytes];data.CopyTo(copy);receive(copy);
   }catch(Exception e){consumers32.Fail();Fail(e);}finally{consumers32.Complete(ticket,done,error,actual);}
  });}catch(Exception e){consumers32.Fail();Fail(e);throw;} // uncertain enqueue: no release/retry
 }
 public void StopRecording(){stopped=true;consumers32.Seal();submission?.Stop();}
 public void ReportFailure(Exception error){stopped=true;Fail(error);}
 public void RequireCompleted(){if(Failure!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("31 source producer failed",Failure);if(!CanUsePixels)throw new InvalidOperationException("31 pending/invalid GPU completion; no output marker");}
 public bool TryReleaseOwned(){
  StopRecording();if(Disposed)return true;if(!CanRelease||releaseAttempted)return false;releaseAttempted=true;
  try{foreach(var t in owned)t?.Release();owned.Clear();if(depthMats!=null)foreach(var row in depthMats)if(row!=null)foreach(var m in row)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);if(qMats!=null)foreach(var m in qMats)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);if(fineMats!=null)foreach(var m in fineMats)if(m)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(m);if(ink)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(ink);if(pigment)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(pigment);if(classify)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(classify);if(area36)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(area36);ReleaseCoupled98();ReleaseJoint97();ReleaseRibbon93();if(inkStyle39)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(inkStyle39);if(hull60)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(hull60);if(contourMesh40)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(contourMesh40);if(outline39)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(outline39);ReleaseFinal41();ReleaseHistory75();Disposed=true;return true;}catch(Exception e){Fail(e);return false;}
 }
 public void Dispose(){TryReleaseOwned();} // Pending means retained, never forced.
}

public sealed class SourceCaptureHost40 {
 // Deliberate bounded strong roots: do not lose pending resources when an
 // external command returns. A retained owner blocks another arm on that view.
 static readonly PendingViews40<SourceCaptureHost40> pendingOwners=new PendingViews40<SourceCaptureHost40>();bool rootReleased;
 public static int PendingOwnerCount=>pendingOwners.Count; // Outer scope must be0 before starting another Play.
 readonly EditorExitRequest40 exitRequest32=new EditorExitRequest40();
 readonly SourceOutline40 producer;readonly Action restoreBeforeExit,restoreAfterEdit;readonly ExitCoordinator40 exit=new ExitCoordinator40();bool armed,stopped;

 void Stop(){stopped=true;RenderPipelineManager.beginCameraRendering-=Enqueue;producer.StopRecording();}
 void BeginExitOnEditor32(){exit.Begin(Stop,restoreBeforeExit,()=>{if(EditorApplication.isPlaying||EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)EditorApplication.ExitPlaymode();});}
 // Called by an explicit external observer. No background polling is installed.
 public void BeginExit(){exitRequest32.Request();}
 public void PumpExit(){exitRequest32.RequireOwner();exitRequest32.Drain(BeginExitOnEditor32);exit.Pump(!EditorApplication.isPlaying&&!EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode&&!EditorApplication.isCompiling&&!EditorApplication.isUpdating,()=>producer.CanRelease,()=>{if(!producer.TryReleaseOwned())throw new InvalidOperationException("31 owned resources retained; release failure");},restoreAfterEdit);if(exit.Finished&&armed&&!rootReleased){pendingOwners.Release(producer.ViewIdentity,this);rootReleased=true;}}
 public bool Ready=>producer.CanUsePixels;public Exception ProducerError=>producer.Failure;public bool ResourcesReleased=>exit.Released;public bool StateRestored=>exit.Restored;public bool ExitAttempted=>exit.ExitAttempted;public string[]Errors=>exit.Errors;
}

public static class ContourPayload44 {
 public sealed class Channels {public Vector3[] a,b;public Vector4[] uv;}
 public static Channels Build(Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor.Contour40.Result source,Vector2[] meshUV){
  int n=source.vertices.Length;if(n%2!=0||source.provenance.Length!=n||source.sourceVertexIndices.Length!=n)throw new ArgumentException("44 paired source line ABI");var r=new Channels{a=new Vector3[n],b=new Vector3[n],uv=new Vector4[n]};
  for(int i=0;i<n;i+=2){int a=source.provenance[i].y==0?i:i+1,b=a==i?i+1:i;if(source.provenance[a].y!=0||source.provenance[b].y!=1||source.provenance[a].x!=source.provenance[b].x)throw new ArgumentException("44 canonical source endpoints required");var ua=meshUV[source.sourceVertexIndices[a]];var ub=meshUV[source.sourceVertexIndices[b]];for(int j=i;j<i+2;j++){r.a[j]=source.vertices[a];r.b[j]=source.vertices[b];r.uv[j]=new Vector4(ua.x,ua.y,ub.x,ub.y);}}
  return r;
 }
}


// Exception handling must never skip the GPU completion path of submitted work.
public static class CompletionScope50 {
 public static void Record(Action candidate,Action<Exception> failed,Action completion){
  try{candidate();}catch(Exception error){failed(error);}finally{completion();}
 }
}

// Cleanup-only completion for submitted work whose final camera never recorded.
// Called only by the existing owner-thread exit pump after recording is stopped.
// Never manufactures a completion, enables pixels, or retries an uncertain API.
public static class FailureCompletion56 {
 public static bool AfterStop(ref bool registered,bool stopped,Submission40 ticket,string key,int generation,
     Action<Action<bool,bool,int>> enqueue,Action<Exception> failed){
  if(!stopped)throw new InvalidOperationException("56 completion drain requires stopped recording");
  if(registered||ticket==null||!ticket.CommandsPossible||ticket.CanRelease)return false;
  registered=true;ticket.Fail();
  try{
   ticket.BeforeBarrier();
   enqueue((done,error,bytes)=>{
    try{bool invalid=error||bytes!=16;ticket.AcceptCallback(key,generation,done,invalid);if(!done||invalid)failed(new InvalidOperationException("56 incomplete/error/size completion; retain resources"));}
    catch(Exception e){failed(e);}
   });
  }catch(Exception e){failed(e);}
  return true;
 }
}

namespace SourceMath67 {
// Pure CPU port of source-palette54. No model/material-role constants or Unity state.
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public static class SourcePalette58
{
    public sealed class Source { public string modelSHA256,imageSHA256,uvOrigin="top-left",encoding="srgb8";public int materialId;public double[] factorLinear; }
    public sealed class Sample { public string id,imageSHA256;public int materialId,primitiveId,triangle;public double[] uv;public int[] texel,rgba8;public double weight; }
    public sealed class Family { public string id,domainId;public Source source;public Sample[] samples; }
    public sealed class Colour { public Sample sample;public double[] linear,lab;public int[] rgb8;public double weight; }
    public sealed class Reference { public int[] rgb8;public double[] lab;public Sample sample;public double cost;public int sampleCount; }
    public sealed class Settings { public int count;public double darkLRatio,darkCRatio,gamma,intervalScale; }
    public sealed class Palette { public int[][] rgb8;public double[] targetL,requestedC,usedC,actualL;public bool[] gamutReduced;public int duplicates,LReversals; }
    public static bool Finite(double v) {return !double.IsNaN(v)&&!double.IsInfinity(v);}
    static void Require(bool ok,string message) {if(!ok)throw new ArgumentException(message);}
    public static double Decode(double x) {return x<=.04045?x/12.92:Math.Pow((x+.055)/1.055,2.4);}
    public static double Encode(double x) {return x<=.0031308?12.92*x:1.055*Math.Pow(x,1.0/2.4)-.055;}
    static double Cbrt(double x) {return Math.Sign(x)*Math.Pow(Math.Abs(x),1.0/3);}
    static int RGB8(double x) {return (int)Math.Floor(255*Encode(x)+.5);}
    public static double[] LabLinear(double[] c) {
        double r=c[0],g=c[1],b=c[2];
        double l=Cbrt(.4122214708*r+.5363325363*g+.0514459929*b),m=Cbrt(.2119034982*r+.6806995451*g+.1073969566*b),s=Cbrt(.0883024619*r+.2817188376*g+.6299787005*b);
        return new[]{.2104542553*l+.793617785*m-.0040720468*s,1.9779984951*l-2.428592205*m+.4505937099*s,.0259040371*l+.7827717662*m-.808675766*s};
    }
    public static double[] LabRGB(int[] rgb) {return LabLinear(rgb.Select(v=>Decode(v/255.0)).ToArray());}
    public static double[] LinearLch(double L,double C,double h) {
        double a=C*Math.Cos(h),b=C*Math.Sin(h),l=Math.Pow(L+.3963377774*a+.2158037573*b,3),m=Math.Pow(L-.1055613458*a-.0638541728*b,3),s=Math.Pow(L-.0894841775*a-1.291485548*b,3);
        return new[]{4.0767416621*l-3.3077115913*m+.2309699292*s,-1.2684380046*l+2.6097574011*m-.3413193965*s,-.0041960863*l-.7034186147*m+1.707614701*s};
    }
    static bool Valid(double[] v) {return v.All(x=>x>=-1e-9&&x<=1+1e-9);}
    public static int[] Gamut(double L,double C,double h,out double usedC) {
        usedC=C;var v=LinearLch(L,C,h);
        if(!Valid(v)) {double lo=0,hi=C;for(int i=0;i<28;i++){double mid=(lo+hi)/2;if(Valid(LinearLch(L,mid,h)))lo=mid;else hi=mid;}usedC=lo;v=LinearLch(L,usedC,h);}
        return v.Select(x=>RGB8(Math.Max(0,Math.Min(1,x)))).ToArray();
    }
    public static Colour[] SourceColours(Family f) {
        Require(f!=null&&f.source!=null&&f.samples!=null,"Explicit source family required");var s=f.source;
        Require(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.modelSHA256)&&!string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.imageSHA256)&&s.uvOrigin=="top-left"&&s.encoding=="srgb8","Original source/encoding required");
        Require(s.factorLinear!=null&&s.factorLinear.Length==4&&s.factorLinear.All(x=>Finite(x)&&x>=0&&x<=1),"Linear material factor required");
        var result=new List<Colour>();
        foreach(var v in f.samples) {
            Require(v.materialId==s.materialId&&v.imageSHA256==s.imageSHA256,"Source family mismatch");
            Require(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(v.id)&&v.uv!=null&&v.uv.Length==2&&v.uv.All(Finite)&&v.texel!=null&&v.texel.Length==2,"Original UV/texel missing");
            Require(v.rgba8!=null&&v.rgba8.Length==4&&v.rgba8.All(x=>x>=0&&x<=255)&&v.weight>0&&Finite(v.weight),"Invalid source sample");
            double weight=v.weight*v.rgba8[3]/255.0*s.factorLinear[3];
            if(weight<=0)continue;
            var linear=Enumerable.Range(0,3).Select(i=>Decode(v.rgba8[i]/255.0)*s.factorLinear[i]).ToArray();
            result.Add(new Colour {sample=v,linear=linear,lab=LabLinear(linear),rgb8=linear.Select(RGB8).ToArray(),weight=weight});
        }
        return result.ToArray();
    }
    public static Reference ExtractReference(Family f) {
        var samples=SourceColours(f);Require(samples.Length>0,"No visible source samples");Colour best=null;double cost=double.PositiveInfinity;
        foreach(var c in samples) {
            double sum=0;foreach(var s in samples){double q=0;for(int i=0;i<3;i++)q+=(s.lab[i]-c.lab[i])*(s.lab[i]-c.lab[i]);sum+=s.weight*q;}
            // Stable original ASCII sample IDs in this provider; see portability limit.
            if(sum<cost||(sum==cost&&(best==null||StringComparer.Ordinal.Compare(c.sample.id,best.sample.id)<0))){best=c;cost=sum;}
        }
        return new Reference {rgb8=(int[])best.rgb8.Clone(),lab=(double[])best.lab.Clone(),sample=best.sample,cost=cost,sampleCount=samples.Length};
    }
    public static Palette MakePalette(Reference reference,Settings p) {
        Require(p.count>=2&&p.count<=256&&Finite(p.darkLRatio)&&Finite(p.darkCRatio)&&p.darkLRatio>=0&&p.darkLRatio<=1&&p.darkCRatio>=0&&p.darkCRatio<=1&&p.gamma>0&&Finite(p.gamma)&&p.intervalScale>=0&&Finite(p.intervalScale),"Invalid palette settings");
        var lab=LabRGB(reference.rgb8);double L0=lab[0],C0=Math.Sqrt(lab[1]*lab[1]+lab[2]*lab[2]),h=Math.Atan2(lab[2],lab[1]);
        var result=new Palette {rgb8=new int[p.count][],targetL=new double[p.count],requestedC=new double[p.count],usedC=new double[p.count],actualL=new double[p.count],gamutReduced=new bool[p.count]};
        for(int k=0;k<p.count;k++) {
            double t=Math.Pow((double)k/(p.count-1),p.gamma),L=L0+p.intervalScale*(L0*(p.darkLRatio+(1-p.darkLRatio)*t)-L0),C=C0+p.intervalScale*(C0*(p.darkCRatio+(1-p.darkCRatio)*t)-C0);
            Require(L>=0&&L<=1&&C>=0,"Palette interval exceeds L/C domain");result.targetL[k]=L;result.requestedC[k]=C;
            if(k==p.count-1||p.intervalScale==0){result.rgb8[k]=(int[])reference.rgb8.Clone();result.usedC[k]=C0;}
            else {double used;result.rgb8[k]=Gamut(L,C,h,out used);result.usedC[k]=used;result.gamutReduced[k]=C-used>1e-7;}
            result.actualL[k]=LabRGB(result.rgb8[k])[0];if(k>0&&result.actualL[k]<result.actualL[k-1]-1e-9)result.LReversals++;
        }
        result.duplicates=p.count-result.rgb8.Select(v=>string.Join(",",v)).Distinct().Count();return result;
    }
    public static int ShadeIndex(double g,double[] thresholds) {
        Require(Finite(g)&&thresholds!=null&&thresholds.All(Finite),"Finite shade inputs required");
        float x=(float)g;Require(!float.IsInfinity(x),"Shade exceeds Float32");int k=0;float previous=float.NegativeInfinity;
        foreach(double value in thresholds){float cut=(float)value;Require(!float.IsInfinity(cut)&&cut>previous,"Sorted distinct Float32 thresholds required");if(x>=cut)k++;previous=cut;}return k;
    }
    public static int[] Resolve(Palette p,int k) {Require(k>=0&&k<p.rgb8.Length,"Unbound shade index");return (int[])p.rgb8[k].Clone();}
}

}

reject whole frame");}}receipt["observedActorCells77"]=actorCount77;receipt["observedFaceCells77"]=faceCount77;if(actorCount77==0)Fail(new InvalidOperationException("77 character not observed; stop and drain, no temporal art claim"));var encoded=FrameBytes77.Encode(bytes);using(var digest=SHA256.Create())receipt["rawSHA256"]=BitConverter.ToString(digest.ComputeHash(bytes)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();receipt["storedBytes"]=encoded.Length;using(var file=new FileStream(Path.Combine(root77,(string)receipt["compressedFile"]),FileMode.CreateNew,FileAccess.Write,FileShare.Read)){file.Write(encoded,0,encoded.Length);file.Flush(true);}lock(sync77){Receipt77=receipt;}Received77?.Invoke(receipt);if(invalid81)Fail(new InvalidOperationException("81 current source renderer ordering invalid; readback completed, stop"));
    }catch(Exception e){Fail(e);}finally{lock(sync77){done77=gpuDone;written77=true;}if(gpuDone&&prior!=null)System.Threading.Interlocked.Decrement(ref prior.Borrowers77);}
   });
  }catch(Exception e){Fail(e);} // An uncertain enqueue stays pending, never resubmitted/released.
 }
}

// Small exact-byte record format; no extra assembly, Unity state or GPU use.
// Header: R77F / uint32 mode(0 raw,1 RLE16) / uint32 raw length, little endian.
// RLE token: high bit = repeated16-byte pixel; other31 bits = positive count.
// Literal token's payload is count*16 bytes. Raw fallback never exceeds raw+12.
public static class FrameBytes77 {
 public static byte[] Encode(byte[] bytes){
  if(bytes==null||bytes.Length==0||bytes.Length>262144||bytes.Length%16!=0)throw new System.ArgumentException("77 bounded RGBAFloat32 bytes");
  int n=bytes.Length/16;bool Same(int a,int b)=>System.BitConverter.ToInt64(bytes,16*a)==System.BitConverter.ToInt64(bytes,16*b)&&System.BitConverter.ToInt64(bytes,16*a+8)==System.BitConverter.ToInt64(bytes,16*b+8);
  byte[] payload;using(var memory=new System.IO.MemoryStream())using(var w=new System.IO.BinaryWriter(memory)){
   int i=0;while(i<n){int run=1;while(i+run<n&&Same(i,i+run))run++;if(run>=2){w.Write(0x80000000u|(uint)run);w.Write(bytes,i*16,16);i+=run;}else{int start=i++;while(i<n&&!(i+1<n&&Same(i,i+1)))i++;w.Write((uint)(i-start));w.Write(bytes,start*16,(i-start)*16);}}payload=memory.ToArray();
  }
  using(var output=new System.IO.MemoryStream())using(var w=new System.IO.BinaryWriter(output)){w.Write(new byte[]{82,55,55,70});w.Write(payload.Length<bytes.Length?1u:0u);w.Write((uint)bytes.Length);w.Write(payload.Length<bytes.Length?payload:bytes);return output.ToArray();}
 }
}

// Pure CPU construction. Final runtime RGB is a finite table lookup only.
// Linear-brightness floor and OKLab L/C ratios have different units. The
// neutral-floor adapter uses cbrt(floor) (OKLab homogeneity), not the same number.
namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
#if CAPTURE89
using P89=SourceMath67.SourcePalette58;
#else
using P89=global::SourcePalette58;
#endif
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Linq;
public static class PaletteRuntime89 {
 public sealed class Result {public byte[] rgb8;public float[] linear;public double floor,labRatio,maxCodeError;public int roundingChanges,sourceContrastPairs92,contrastCorrections92;}
 static void Need(bool b,string why){if(!b)throw new ArgumentException(why);}
 internal static double Y(int rgb){return .2126*P89.Decode((rgb>>16)/255.0)+.7152*P89.Decode(((rgb>>8)&255)/255.0)+.0722*P89.Decode((rgb&255)/255.0);}
 static int Key(int r,int g,int b){return(r<<16)|(g<<8)|b;}
 static int Channel(int key,int j){return(key>>(16-8*j))&255;}
 public static Result Build(byte[] references,int[] pairs,int levels,double floor){
  Need(references!=null&&references.Length>0&&references.Length%3==0&&references.Length<=16384*3&&pairs!=null&&pairs.Length%2==0&&pairs.Length<=131072&&levels>=2&&levels<=256&&P89.Finite(floor)&&floor>=0&&floor<=1,"89 bounded source/contrast/floor ABI");
  int n=references.Length/3;var next=new List<int>[n];var previous=new List<int>[n];var degree=new int[n];for(int i=0;i<n;i++){next[i]=new List<int>();previous[i]=new List<int>();}var unique=new Dictionary<long,byte>();
  for(int j=0;j<pairs.Length;j+=2){int a=pairs[j],b=pairs[j+1];Need(a>=0&&b>=0&&a<n&&b<n&&a!=b,"89 source contrast endpoint");long edgeKey=((long)a<<32)|(uint)b;if(!unique.ContainsKey(edgeKey)){unique.Add(edgeKey,0);next[a].Add(b);previous[b].Add(a);degree[b]++;}}
  var ready=new List<int>();for(int i=0;i<n;i++)if(degree[i]==0)ready.Add(i);var order=new List<int>();while(ready.Count>0){int i=ready[0];foreach(int item in ready)i=Math.Min(i,item);ready.Remove(i);order.Add(i);foreach(int j in next[i])if(--degree[j]==0)ready.Add(j);}Need(order.Count==n,"89 cyclic contrast graph");
  double ratio=Math.Pow(floor,1.0/3.0);var settings=new P89.Settings{count=levels,darkLRatio=ratio,darkCRatio=ratio,gamma=1,intervalScale=1};var target=new double[n,levels,3];var old=new byte[n*levels*3];
  for(int i=0;i<n;i++){var rgb=new[]{(int)references[3*i],(int)references[3*i+1],(int)references[3*i+2]};var lab=P89.LabRGB(rgb);var p=P89.MakePalette(new P89.Reference{rgb8=rgb},settings);for(int k=0;k<levels;k++){var c=P89.LinearLch(p.targetL[k],p.usedC[k],Math.Atan2(lab[2],lab[1]));for(int j=0;j<3;j++){target[i,k,j]=k==levels-1?rgb[j]:Math.Max(0,Math.Min(255,255*P89.Encode(c[j])));old[(i*levels+k)*3+j]=(byte)p.rgb8[k][j];}}}
  var result=new Result{rgb8=new byte[n*levels*3],linear=new float[n*levels*4],floor=floor,labRatio=ratio};var shared=new Dictionary<int,double>();
  for(int k=0;k<levels;k++){
   var keys=new int[n][];var values=new double[n][];
   for(int i=0;i<n;i++){var combinations=new List<int>();for(int a=0;a<8;a++){int r=(int)((a&1)==0?Math.Floor(target[i,k,0]):Math.Ceiling(target[i,k,0])),g=(int)((a&2)==0?Math.Floor(target[i,k,1]):Math.Ceiling(target[i,k,1])),b=(int)((a&4)==0?Math.Floor(target[i,k,2]):Math.Ceiling(target[i,k,2]));int encoded=Key(r,g,b);if(!combinations.Contains(encoded))combinations.Add(encoded);}combinations.Sort();keys[i]=combinations.ToArray();values[i]=new double[keys[i].Length];for(int j=0;j<keys[i].Length;j++){double y;if(!shared.TryGetValue(keys[i][j],out y)){y=Y(keys[i][j]);shared.Add(keys[i][j],y);}values[i][j]=y;}}
   var upper=new double[n];for(int j=order.Count-1;j>=0;j--){int i=order[j];double bound=Double.PositiveInfinity;foreach(int q in next[i])bound=Math.Min(bound,upper[q]);double best=Double.NegativeInfinity;foreach(double y in values[i])if(y<bound)best=Math.Max(best,y);Need(!Double.IsNegativeInfinity(best),"89 floor/ceil domains cannot preserve observed source contrast; do not change targets or submit GPU");upper[i]=best;}
   var selected=new double[n];foreach(int i in order){double lower=Double.NegativeInfinity;foreach(int p in previous[i])lower=Math.Max(lower,selected[p]);int key=-1;double cost=Double.PositiveInfinity,yChosen=0;for(int j=0;j<keys[i].Length;j++){double y=values[i][j];if(y<=lower||y>upper[i])continue;double error=0;for(int c=0;c<3;c++){double d=Channel(keys[i][j],c)-target[i,k,c];error+=d*d;}if(error<cost||(error==cost&&keys[i][j]<key)){key=keys[i][j];cost=error;yChosen=y;}}Need(key>=0,"89 exhausted palette domain");selected[i]=yChosen;bool changed=false;for(int c=0;c<3;c++){byte b=(byte)Channel(key,c);result.rgb8[(i*levels+k)*3+c]=b;result.linear[(i*levels+k)*4+c]=(float)P89.Decode(b/255.0);result.maxCodeError=Math.Max(result.maxCodeError,Math.Abs(b-target[i,k,c]));changed|=b!=old[(i*levels+k)*3+c];}result.linear[(i*levels+k)*4+3]=1;if(changed)result.roundingChanges++;}
   foreach(long e in unique.Keys){int a=(int)(e>>32),b=(int)(e&0xFFFFFFFF);Need(selected[a]<selected[b],"89 palette source-order invariant");}
  }
  Need(result.maxCodeError<1+1e-10,"89 palette exceeds floor/ceil quantization domain");return result;
 }
 // Optional measured-RGB constraints are CPU preprocessing only. The original
 // four-argument builder remains unchanged and reproduces the actual89 table.
 public static Result BuildConstrained92(byte[] references,int[] pairs,int levels,double floor,int[] nativeContrasts){
  var original=Build(references,pairs,levels,floor);int n=references.Length/3;var target=new double[n,levels,3];double ratio=Math.Pow(floor,1.0/3.0);var settings=new P89.Settings{count=levels,darkLRatio=ratio,darkCRatio=ratio,gamma=1,intervalScale=1};
  for(int i=0;i<n;i++){var rgb=new[]{(int)references[3*i],(int)references[3*i+1],(int)references[3*i+2]};var lab=P89.LabRGB(rgb);var palette=P89.MakePalette(new P89.Reference{rgb8=rgb},settings);for(int k=0;k<levels;k++){var c=P89.LinearLch(palette.targetL[k],palette.usedC[k],Math.Atan2(lab[2],lab[1]));for(int j=0;j<3;j++)target[i,k,j]=k==levels-1?rgb[j]:Math.Max(0,Math.Min(255,255*P89.Encode(c[j])));}}
  var before=original.rgb8;var corrected=PaletteConstraints92.Solve(target,before,pairs,nativeContrasts);original.rgb8=corrected;original.maxCodeError=0;original.sourceContrastPairs92=nativeContrasts.Length/5;
  for(int i=0;i<n;i++)for(int k=0;k<levels;k++){bool changed=false;for(int j=0;j<3;j++){int at=(i*levels+k)*3+j;changed|=corrected[at]!=before[at];original.maxCodeError=Math.Max(original.maxCodeError,Math.Abs(corrected[at]-target[i,k,j]));original.linear[(i*levels+k)*4+j]=(float)P89.Decode(corrected[at]/255.0);}if(changed)original.contrastCorrections92++;}
  return original;
 }
}
}

// CPU-only, bounded RGB8 feasibility. No shader, readback, Unity static cache,
// fitted RGB, light multiplication or artist-facing heuristic is introduced.
namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
using System;using System.Collections.Generic;
public static class PaletteConstraints92 {
 struct Edge {public int a,b;public Edge(int a,int b){this.a=a;this.b=b;}}
 sealed class Problem {
  public int n;public int[][] colour,rank;public List<int>[] adjacent;public Dictionary<int,bool[,]> relations=new Dictionary<int,bool[,]>();public long revisions;public int nodes;
  public bool[][] Copy(bool[][] d){var r=new bool[n][];for(int i=0;i<n;i++)r[i]=(bool[])d[i].Clone();return r;}
  public void Add(int a,int b,bool[,] allowed){if(a<0||a>=n||b<0||b>=n||a==b)throw new ArgumentException("92 contrast endpoints");if(a>b){int c=a;a=b;b=c;var t=new bool[allowed.GetLength(1),allowed.GetLength(0)];for(int i=0;i<t.GetLength(0);i++)for(int j=0;j<t.GetLength(1);j++)t[i,j]=allowed[j,i];allowed=t;}int key=a*n+b;bool[,] old;if(relations.TryGetValue(key,out old)){for(int i=0;i<old.GetLength(0);i++)for(int j=0;j<old.GetLength(1);j++)old[i,j]&=allowed[i,j];}else{relations.Add(key,allowed);adjacent[a].Add(b);adjacent[b].Add(a);}}
  bool Allowed(int a,int b,int i,int j){return a<b?relations[a*n+b][i,j]:relations[b*n+a][j,i];}
  public bool Propagate(bool[][] domains,Queue<Edge> queue){while(queue.Count>0){if(++revisions>2000000)throw new ArgumentException("92 CPU arc budget; no GPU submit");var e=queue.Dequeue();bool changed=false,any=false;for(int i=0;i<domains[e.a].Length;i++){if(!domains[e.a][i])continue;bool supported=false;for(int j=0;j<domains[e.b].Length;j++)if(domains[e.b][j]&&Allowed(e.a,e.b,i,j)){supported=true;break;}if(!supported){domains[e.a][i]=false;changed=true;}else any=true;}if(!any)return false;if(changed)foreach(int q in adjacent[e.a])if(q!=e.b)queue.Enqueue(new Edge(q,e.a));}return true;}
  public int[] Search(){var initial=new bool[n][];var queue=new Queue<Edge>();for(int i=0;i<n;i++){initial[i]=new bool[colour[i].Length];for(int j=0;j<initial[i].Length;j++)initial[i][j]=true;foreach(int b in adjacent[i])queue.Enqueue(new Edge(i,b));}if(!Propagate(initial,queue))throw new ArgumentException("92 measured colour/Y constraints infeasible");var stack=new Stack<bool[][]>();stack.Push(initial);
   while(stack.Count>0){if(++nodes>4096)throw new ArgumentException("92 CPU search budget; no GPU submit");var d=stack.Pop();int chosen=-1,best=Int32.MaxValue;for(int i=0;i<n;i++){int count=0;foreach(bool b in d[i])if(b)count++;if(count>1&&(count<best||(count==best&&(chosen<0||adjacent[i].Count>adjacent[chosen].Count)))){chosen=i;best=count;}}
    if(chosen<0){var result=new int[n];for(int i=0;i<n;i++)for(int j=0;j<d[i].Length;j++)if(d[i][j])result[i]=colour[i][j];foreach(var kv in relations){int a=kv.Key/n,b=kv.Key%n;int ai=Array.IndexOf(colour[a],result[a]),bi=Array.IndexOf(colour[b],result[b]);if(!kv.Value[ai,bi])throw new InvalidOperationException("92 output constraint mismatch");}return result;}
    // Push reverse preference order: base89 first, then original target error
    // and canonical RGB. This is feasibility, not a global SSE optimum.
    for(int r=rank[chosen].Length-1;r>=0;r--){int at=rank[chosen][r];if(!d[chosen][at])continue;var child=Copy(d);for(int j=0;j<child[chosen].Length;j++)child[chosen][j]=j==at;queue=new Queue<Edge>();foreach(int q in adjacent[chosen])queue.Enqueue(new Edge(q,chosen));if(Propagate(child,queue))stack.Push(child);}
   }throw new ArgumentException("92 measured colour/Y constraints infeasible after complete search");
  }
 }
 static int Channel(int key,int c){return(key>>(16-8*c))&255;}
 public static byte[] Solve(double[,,] target,byte[] baseline,int[] orders,int[] vectors){
  int n=target.GetLength(0),levels=target.GetLength(1);if(n<1||n>1024||target.GetLength(2)!=3||baseline==null||baseline.Length!=n*levels*3||orders==null||orders.Length%2!=0||orders.Length>65536||vectors==null||vectors.Length%5!=0||vectors.Length>65536*5)throw new ArgumentException("92 bounded CPU palette ABI");var output=new byte[baseline.Length];
  for(int k=0;k<levels;k++){var p=new Problem{n=n,colour=new int[n][],rank=new int[n][],adjacent=new List<int>[n]};var shared=new Dictionary<int,double>();
   for(int i=0;i<n;i++){var colours=new List<int>();for(int bits=0;bits<8;bits++){int key=0;for(int c=0;c<3;c++){double v=target[i,k,c];if(Double.IsNaN(v)||Double.IsInfinity(v)||v<0||v>255)throw new ArgumentException("92 finite target");int v8=(int)((bits&(1<<c))==0?Math.Floor(v):Math.Ceiling(v));key|=v8<<(16-8*c);}if(!colours.Contains(key))colours.Add(key);}colours.Sort();p.colour[i]=colours.ToArray();p.adjacent[i]=new List<int>();var rank=new List<int>();for(int j=0;j<colours.Count;j++){rank.Add(j);if(!shared.ContainsKey(colours[j]))shared.Add(colours[j],PaletteRuntime89.Y(colours[j]));}int ii=i,baseKey=(baseline[(i*levels+k)*3]<<16)|(baseline[(i*levels+k)*3+1]<<8)|baseline[(i*levels+k)*3+2];rank.Sort((a,b)=>{int first=(colours[a]==baseKey?0:1).CompareTo(colours[b]==baseKey?0:1);if(first!=0)return first;double ca=0,cb=0;for(int c=0;c<3;c++){double da=Channel(colours[a],c)-target[ii,k,c],db=Channel(colours[b],c)-target[ii,k,c];ca+=da*da;cb+=db*db;}int cost=ca.CompareTo(cb);return cost!=0?cost:colours[a].CompareTo(colours[b]);});p.rank[i]=rank.ToArray();}
   for(int j=0;j<orders.Length;j+=2){int a=orders[j],b=orders[j+1];if(a<0||a>=n||b<0||b>=n||a==b)throw new ArgumentException("92 Y endpoints");var allow=new bool[p.colour[a].Length,p.colour[b].Length];for(int x=0;x<p.colour[a].Length;x++)for(int y=0;y<p.colour[b].Length;y++)allow[x,y]=shared[p.colour[a][x]]<shared[p.colour[b][y]];p.Add(a,b,allow);}
   for(int j=0;j<vectors.Length;j+=5){int a=vectors[j],b=vectors[j+1];if(a<0||a>=n||b<0||b>=n||a==b)throw new ArgumentException("92 RGB endpoints");for(int c=2;c<5;c++)if(vectors[j+c]<-255||vectors[j+c]>255)throw new ArgumentException("92 native RGB difference");if(vectors[j+2]==0&&vectors[j+3]==0&&vectors[j+4]==0)throw new ArgumentException("92 zero colour difference");var allow=new bool[p.colour[a].Length,p.colour[b].Length];for(int x=0;x<p.colour[a].Length;x++)for(int y=0;y<p.colour[b].Length;y++){int dot=0;for(int c=0;c<3;c++)dot+=(Channel(p.colour[a][x],c)-Channel(p.colour[b][y],c))*vectors[j+2+c];allow[x,y]=dot>0;}p.Add(a,b,allow);}
   var chosen=p.Search();for(int i=0;i<n;i++)for(int c=0;c<3;c++){int value=Channel(chosen[i],c);if(Math.Abs(value-target[i,k,c])>=1+1e-10)throw new InvalidOperationException("92 palette outside original floor/ceil target");output[(i*levels+k)*3+c]=(byte)value;}
  }return output;
 }
}
}

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public sealed class SourceFiles89 {public string table="TABLE89.json",reference="REFERENCE88.rgb",order="ORDERS88.i32",address="ADDRESS89.r32",control="PALETTE88.rgb",contrast=null;}
public sealed class SourceField89:IDisposable {
 public Texture2D address,control,palette;public readonly Texture original;

 static string Hash89(byte[] b){using(var h=SHA256.Create())return BitConverter.ToString(h.ComputeHash(b)).Replace("-","").ToLowerInvariant();}

  var oldLinear=new float[old.Length/3*4];for(int i=0;i<old.Length/3;i++){for(int c=0;c<3;c++)oldLinear[4*i+c]=(float)SourceMath67.SourcePalette58.Decode(old[3*i+c]/255.0);oldLinear[4*i+3]=1;}
  try{
