   control=new Texture2D(levels,refs.Length/3,TextureFormat.RGBAFloat,false,true){name="67 owned source89 old-unit control LUT",hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave,filterMode=FilterMode.Point};control.SetPixelData(oldLinear,0);control.Apply(false,true);
   palette=new Texture2D(levels,refs.Length/3,TextureFormat.RGBAFloat,false,true){name="67 owned source89 unit-adapted original-colour LUT",hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave,filterMode=FilterMode.Point};palette.SetPixelData(result.linear,0);palette.Apply(false,true);
   using(var w=new FileStream(Path.Combine(evidence,"RUNTIME_PALETTE89.rgb"),FileMode.CreateNew))w.Write(result.rgb8,0,result.rgb8.Length);
   CreateOnly40.Text(Path.Combine(evidence,"RUNTIME_PALETTE89.json"),Info().ToString());
  }catch{Dispose();throw;}
 }
 public void Validate(){if(disposed||!address||!control||!palette||!original||!sourceMaterial||sourceMaterial.GetTexture("_MainTex")!=original||sourceMaterial.GetColor("_Color")!=Color.white||sourceMaterial.GetTextureScale("_MainTex")!=Vector2.one||sourceMaterial.GetTextureOffset("_MainTex")!=Vector2.zero||original.updateCount!=updates||original.imageContentsHash.ToString()!=imageHash||eye.full.faceShadeSteps!=levels||eye.full.motion.animation.shadeProfile.x!=floor||!eye.full.layeredSurface||!eye.full.styleEnabled||!eye.full.shadeLayer)throw new InvalidOperationException("89 source/levels/style changed; invalidate, no stale colour table");}
 public Obj40 Info()=>new Obj40{["sourceValueAddressCount"]=result.rgb8.Length/(3*levels),["sourceContrastPairs92"]=result.sourceContrastPairs92,["contrastCorrections92"]=result.contrastCorrections92,["sourceContract"]=spec,["liveLinearFloor"]=floor,["adaptedLabRatio"]=result.labRatio,["levels"]=levels,["roundingChanges"]=result.roundingChanges,["maximumTargetCodeError"]=result.maxCodeError,["paletteSHA"]=Hash89(result.rgb8),["liveOriginalImageHash"]=imageHash,["liveOriginalUpdateCount"]=updates,["originalFormat"]=original.graphicsFormat.ToString(),["originalHuePreservingCandidate"]=true,["legacyFaceTintReapplied"]=false,["currentLegacyFaceTint"]=eye.full.faceShadeTint,["currentLegacyFaceChromaticity"]=new Arr40(eye.full.faceShadeChromaticity.x,eye.full.faceShadeChromaticity.y,eye.full.faceShadeChromaticity.z),["finalColourOperation"]="finite-LUT Load, current native alpha retained; no RGB/light multiplication or temporal RGB average",["GPUSelectionReadback"]=false,["ownedTextureBytes"]=(long)address.width*address.height*4+(long)palette.width*palette.height*32};
 public void Dispose(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;if(address)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(address);if(control)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(control);if(palette)UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(palette);address=control=palette=null;/* original borrowed */}
}

  for(int i=0;i<ribbonFields93.Length;i++)ribbonFields93[i]=Target(W77,W77);readbackPack93=Target(W77*24,W77);
 }
 void PrepareRibbon93(){
  if(!camera.orthographic||SystemInfo.graphicsDeviceType!=GraphicsDeviceType.Direct3D11||!float.IsFinite(width)||width<0)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 finite orthographic D3D11 native width contract");long tris=meshes.Sum(m=>Enumerable.Range(0,m.subMeshCount).Sum(s=>(long)m.GetIndexCount(s)/3));if(tris>65536||originals.Sum(v=>v.Length)>16)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 source alpha raster budget");foreach(var row in depthMats)foreach(var m in row)if(!m||m.FindPass("SourceDepth29")!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 native alpha/cull/depth ABI changed");
  var mesh=meshes[renderer];var local=mesh.vertices;var normal=mesh.normals;var uv=mesh.uv;var tri=mesh.GetIndices(submesh);var matrix=matrices[renderer];var nm=matrix.inverse.transpose;if(normal.Length!=local.Length||uv.Length!=local.Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 missing current source normals/UV, no substitute");var curve=Contour40.Build(local,tri,weld40,matrix,eye.full.Project);int count=curve.indices.Length/2;if(count<1||count>1024)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 source segment budget");
  var anchors=tri.Select(i=>weld40[i]).Distinct().OrderBy(i=>i).ToArray();var reps=new Dictionary<int,int>();foreach(int i in tri)if(!reps.ContainsKey(weld40[i]))reps.Add(weld40[i],i);double ax=0,ay=0;foreach(int id in anchors){var p=eye.full.Project(matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(local[reps[id]]));ax+=p.x;ay+=p.y;}ax/=anchors.Length;ay/=anchors.Length;if(ax==Math.Floor(ax)||ay==Math.Floor(ay))throw new InvalidOperationException("93 exact half-anchor alternative, no invented first-frame tie");continuous93=new Vector2((float)ax,(float)ay);snapped93=new Vector2((float)Math.Floor(ax)+.5f,(float)Math.Floor(ay)+.5f);

  int n=count*6;var position=new Vector3[n];var corner=new List<Vector3>(n);var aa=new List<Vector3>(n);var bb=new List<Vector3>(n);var na=new List<Vector3>(n);var nb=new List<Vector3>(n);var tex=new List<Vector4>(n);var incident=new List<Vector2>(n);var index=new int[n];int budget=0;var input=new List<float[]>();var corners=new[]{new Vector2(0,0),new Vector2(1,0),new Vector2(1,1),new Vector2(0,0),new Vector2(1,1),new Vector2(0,1)};
  for(int edge=0;edge<count;edge++){
   int i=2*edge,ia=curve.sourceVertexIndices[i],ib=curve.sourceVertexIndices[i+1];if(weld40[ia]>weld40[ib]){int swap=ia;ia=ib;ib=swap;}var a=matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(local[ia]);var b=matrix.MultiplyPoint3x4(local[ib]);var an=nm.MultiplyVector(normal[ia]);var bn=nm.MultiplyVector(normal[ib]);if(!float.IsFinite(an.x)||!float.IsFinite(an.y)||!float.IsFinite(an.z)||!float.IsFinite(bn.x)||!float.IsFinite(bn.y)||!float.IsFinite(bn.z)||an.sqrMagnitude==0||bn.sqrMagnitude==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 finite original deformed world normals");an=an.normalized;bn=bn.normalized;
   uint wa=(uint)weld40[ia],wb=(uint)weld40[ib];var faces=adjacent[((ulong)Math.Min(wa,wb)<<32)|Math.Max(wa,wb)];if(faces.Count!=2)throw new InvalidOperationException("93 current registered manifold edge required");var inc=new Vector2(faces[0],faces[1]);var u=new Vector4(uv[ia].x,uv[ia].y,uv[ib].x,uv[ib].y);float eid=curve.provenance[i].x;
   // UNorm green is in[0,1]. Orthographic projection of each endpoint lies

   // raw and common-anchor variants before any GPU command is recorded.

   for(int c=0;c<6;c++){int at=edge*6+c;index[at]=at;corner.Add(new Vector3(corners[c].x,corners[c].y,eid));aa.Add(a);bb.Add(b);na.Add(an);nb.Add(bn);tex.Add(u);incident.Add(inc);}
  }
  if(!ribbonMesh93)ribbonMesh93=new UnityEngine.Mesh{name="31 owned native ribbon93 mesh",hideFlags=HideFlags.HideAndDontSave};ribbonMesh93.Clear();ribbonMesh93.vertices=position;ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(0,corner);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(2,aa);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(3,bb);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(4,tex);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(5,incident);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(6,na);ribbonMesh93.SetUVs(7,nb);ribbonMesh93.SetIndices(index,MeshTopology.Triangles,0);ribbonMesh93.bounds=new Bounds(Vector3.zero,Vector3.one*10000);view=camera.worldToCameraMatrix;qvp=GL.GetGPUProjectionMatrix(Projection40.Crop(QROI,eye.full.effectiveProjection),true)*view;

namespace Unity.AI.Assistant.Agent.Dynamic.Extension.Editor {
public static class AlphaChannel96 {
 // Format names do not define byte order on every host. Resolve against the
 // public logical alpha getter; never write or dispose the borrowed CPU view.
 public static int Find(Texture2D t,NativeArray<byte> data,int stride){
  if(stride==3)return -1;
  int options=15,pixels=checked(t.width*t.height);
  for(int i=0;i<pixels&&(options&(options-1))!=0;i++){
   int first=-1;bool different=false;
   for(int c=0;c<4;c++)if((options&(1<<c))!=0){int value=data[stride*i+c];if(first<0)first=value;else if(value!=first)different=true;}
   if(!different)continue;int alpha=Mathf.RoundToInt(t.GetPixel(i%t.width,i/t.width,0).a*255);
   for(int c=0;c<4;c++)if((options&(1<<c))!=0&&data[stride*i+c]!=alpha)options&=~(1<<c);
   if(options==0)throw new InvalidOperationException("96 CPU alpha channel not representable by native8bit byte");
  }
  int channel=0;while((options&(1<<channel))==0)channel++;
  for(int k=0;k<17;k++){int i=(int)((long)k*(pixels-1)/16);if(data[stride*i+channel]!=Mathf.RoundToInt(t.GetPixel(i%t.width,i/t.width,0).a*255))throw new InvalidOperationException("96 public alpha calibration differs");}
  return channel;
 }
}
}



namespace LineGeometry97 {
 public delegate void FragmentVisitor97(Triangle strip,int edge,List<P> polygon,bool covered,int x,int y,bool visible,P witness);
 public struct P {
  public double x,y;public P(double x,double y){this.x=x;this.y=y;}
  public static P operator+(P a,P b){return new P(a.x+b.x,a.y+b.y);}public static P operator-(P a,P b){return new P(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);}public static P operator*(P a,double b){return new P(a.x*b,a.y*b);}public static double Dot(P a,P b){return a.x*b.x+a.y*b.y;}public static double Cross(P a,P b){return a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x;}public double Length{get{return Math.Sqrt(x*x+y*y);}}
 }
 public sealed class Image {
  public readonly int width,height,filter,wrapU,wrapV;public byte[] alpha;readonly int globalMin,globalMax;
  public Image(int w,int h,int filter,int u,int v,byte[] a){if(w<1||h<1||a==null||a.Length!=(long)w*h||filter<0||filter>1||u<0||u>2||v<0||v>2)throw new ArgumentException("97 source alpha ABI");width=w;height=h;this.filter=filter;wrapU=u;wrapV=v;alpha=a;int low=255,high=0;foreach(byte value in a){low=Math.Min(low,value);high=Math.Max(high,value);}globalMin=low;globalMax=high;}
  int Wrap(int p,int n,int kind){if(kind==0){p%=n;return p<0?p+n:p;}if(kind==1)return Math.Max(0,Math.Min(n-1,p));if(kind==2){p%=2*n;if(p<0)p+=2*n;return p<n?p:2*n-1-p;}throw new InvalidOperationException("97 unsupported source wrap");}
  public int Range(P lo,P hi,double multiplier,double cutoff,bool blend){
   if(alpha==null)throw new ObjectDisposedException("97 source alpha cache");double offset=filter==0?0:.5;int x0=(int)Math.Floor(lo.x*width-offset),x1=(int)Math.Floor(hi.x*width-offset)+(filter==0?0:1),y0=(int)Math.Floor(lo.y*height-offset),y1=(int)Math.Floor(hi.y*height-offset)+(filter==0?0:1);
   if((long)(x1-x0+1)*(y1-y0+1)>65536||multiplier<0)return 0;int low=255,high=0;
   if(globalMin==globalMax){low=globalMin;high=globalMax;}
   else for(int y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(int x=x0;x<=x1;x++){int a=alpha[Wrap(y,height,wrapV)*width+Wrap(x,width,wrapU)];low=Math.Min(low,a);high=Math.Max(high,a);if(blend&&high*multiplier>0||!blend&&low/255.0*multiplier<=cutoff+1.0/1024&&high/255.0*multiplier>=cutoff-1.0/1024)return 0;}
   // One half-precision ULP around the cutoff is uncertainty, not a depth
   // bias. All actual pixel values remain original source alpha.
   double minimum=low/255.0*multiplier,maximum=high/255.0*multiplier,roundoff=1.0/1024;
   if(blend)return maximum==0?-1:0;
   if(minimum-cutoff>roundoff)return 1;if(cutoff-maximum>roundoff)return -1;return 0;
  }
 }
 public sealed class Material {
  public Image image;public bool test,blend,unresolved;public int cull;public double alpha=1,cutoff;public P scale=new P(1,1),offset;
 }
 public sealed class Triangle {
  public P a,b,c,ab,ac,uvA,uvB,uvC,gradient;public double za,zb,zc,det,zConstant,xyError;public readonly Material material;double lengthAB,lengthBC,lengthCA;
  public Triangle(P a,P b,P c,double za,double zb,double zc,P ua,P ub,P uc,Material m){material=m;Update(a,b,c,za,zb,zc,ua,ub,uc);}
  public void Update(P a,P b,P c,double za,double zb,double zc,P ua,P ub,P uc){this.a=a;this.b=b;this.c=c;ab=b-a;ac=c-a;det=P.Cross(ab,ac);this.za=za;this.zb=zb;this.zc=zc;uvA=ua;uvB=ub;uvC=uc;lengthAB=ab.Length;lengthBC=(c-b).Length;lengthCA=ac.Length;if(det==0){gradient=new P();zConstant=za;}else{gradient=new P(((zb-za)*ac.y-(zc-za)*ab.y)/det,(ab.x*(zc-za)-ac.x*(zb-za))/det);zConstant=za-P.Dot(gradient,a);}}
  public void ExpandedBounds99(double error,out P lo,out P hi){double area=Math.Abs(det),du=error*lengthCA/area,dv=error*lengthAB/area,dw=error*lengthBC/area;P first=a-ab*du-ac*dv,second=a+ab*(1+dv+dw)-ac*dv,third=a-ab*du+ac*(1+du+dw);lo=new P(Math.Min(first.x,Math.Min(second.x,third.x)),Math.Min(first.y,Math.Min(second.y,third.y)));hi=new P(Math.Max(first.x,Math.Max(second.x,third.x)),Math.Max(first.y,Math.Max(second.y,third.y)));}
  public double Outside(P p){double sign=det>0?1:-1;return Math.Max(-sign*P.Cross(ab,p-a)/lengthAB,Math.Max(-sign*P.Cross(c-b,p-b)/lengthBC,-sign*P.Cross(a-c,p-c)/lengthCA));}
  static bool CutSegment(double first,double last,ref double lo,ref double hi){if(first<0&&last<0)return false;if((first<0)!=(last<0)){double cut=first/(first-last);if(first<0)lo=Math.Max(lo,cut);else hi=Math.Min(hi,cut);}return lo<=hi;}
  public bool SegmentInterval(P first,P last,double margin,out double lo,out double hi){lo=0;hi=1;double sign=det>0?1:-1;return CutSegment(sign*P.Cross(ab,first-a)+margin*lengthAB,sign*P.Cross(ab,last-a)+margin*lengthAB,ref lo,ref hi)&&CutSegment(sign*P.Cross(c-b,first-b)+margin*lengthBC,sign*P.Cross(c-b,last-b)+margin*lengthBC,ref lo,ref hi)&&CutSegment(sign*P.Cross(a-c,first-c)+margin*lengthCA,sign*P.Cross(a-c,last-c)+margin*lengthCA,ref lo,ref hi);}
  public double Z(P p){return P.Dot(gradient,p)+zConstant;}
  public void Bary(P p,out double u,out double v){P d=p-a;u=P.Cross(d,ac)/det;v=P.Cross(ab,d)/det;}
  public int Alpha(P p,double radius){var m=material;if(m.unresolved)return 0;if(!m.test&&!m.blend)return 1;if(m.image==null)return 0;double u,v;Bary(p,out u,out v);P uv=(uvA+(uvB-uvA)*u+(uvC-uvA)*v);uv=new P(uv.x*m.scale.x+m.offset.x,uv.y*m.scale.y+m.offset.y);
   P du=new P((ac.y*(uvB.x-uvA.x)-ab.y*(uvC.x-uvA.x))/det,(ab.x*(uvC.x-uvA.x)-ac.x*(uvB.x-uvA.x))/det),dv=new P((ac.y*(uvB.y-uvA.y)-ab.y*(uvC.y-uvA.y))/det,(ab.x*(uvC.y-uvA.y)-ac.x*(uvB.y-uvA.y))/det);P e=new P((Math.Abs(du.x)+Math.Abs(du.y))*radius*Math.Abs(m.scale.x),(Math.Abs(dv.x)+Math.Abs(dv.y))*radius*Math.Abs(m.scale.y));return m.image.Range(uv-e,uv+e,m.alpha,m.cutoff,m.blend);
  }
  public bool Culled{get{return det==0||(material.cull==2&&det<0)||(material.cull==1&&det>0);}}
 }
 public struct Probe {public int state;public double clearance;public Probe(int s,double c){state=s;clearance=c;}}
 public sealed class Scene {
  public P origin;readonly int width;readonly List<Triangle>[] cells;readonly List<Triangle>[] fineCells99;public int fineEntries99;public int visits,queries,entries;readonly HashSet<Triangle> segmentCandidates=new HashSet<Triangle>();
  const double FloatEpsilon=1.1920928955078125e-7;
  readonly int ownerThread103=System.Threading.Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId;readonly Scene parent103;int readers103,generation103,leaseGeneration103;bool released103;
  Scene(Scene parent){parent.Owner103();parent103=parent;width=parent.width;origin=parent.origin;cells=parent.cells;fineCells99=parent.fineCells99;entries=parent.entries;fineEntries99=parent.fineEntries99;leaseGeneration103=parent.generation103;parent.readers103++;}
  void Owner103(){if(System.Threading.Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId!=ownerThread103)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 scene mutations belong to creator thread");}
  void QueryScope103(){if(parent103==null)Owner103();else if(released103||parent103.generation103!=leaseGeneration103)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 expired read-only query view");}
  public Scene ForkQueries103(){Owner103();if(parent103!=null)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 query views cannot fork");return new Scene(this);}
  public void ReleaseQueries103(){if(parent103==null)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 main scene is not a query lease");parent103.Owner103();if(released103)return;released103=true;parent103.readers103--;if(parent103.readers103<0)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 query lease underflow");}
  public int ActiveQueryViews103{get{return readers103;}}
  public Scene(List<Triangle> triangles,P origin,int width){if(width<1||width>64||triangles.Count>65536)throw new ArgumentException("97 source scene limits");this.width=width;cells=new List<Triangle>[width*width];fineCells99=new List<Triangle>[width*width*4];Rebuild(triangles,origin);}
  public void Rebuild(List<Triangle> triangles,P origin){Owner103();if(parent103!=null||readers103!=0)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 cannot rebuild with query views");generation103++;if(triangles.Count>65536)throw new ArgumentException("97 source triangles");this.origin=origin;visits=queries=0;foreach(var cell in cells)if(cell!=null)cell.Clear();foreach(var cell in fineCells99)if(cell!=null)cell.Clear();int count=0,fineCount99=0;
   foreach(var t in triangles){if(t.Culled)continue;double e=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.a.x),Math.Abs(t.a.y)),Math.Max(Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.b.x),Math.Abs(t.b.y)),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.c.x),Math.Abs(t.c.y)))));int x0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(t.a.x,Math.Min(t.b.x,t.c.x))-e-origin.x)),x1=Math.Min(width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(t.a.x,Math.Max(t.b.x,t.c.x))+e-origin.x)),y0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(t.a.y,Math.Min(t.b.y,t.c.y))-e-origin.y)),y1=Math.Min(width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(t.a.y,Math.Max(t.b.y,t.c.y))+e-origin.y));
    for(int y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(int x=x0;x<=x1;x++){if(++count>262144)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 CPU spatial index budget before GPU submit");int at=y*width+x;if(cells[at]==null)cells[at]=new List<Triangle>();cells[at].Add(t);}
    if(x0<=x1&&y0<=y1){double maximumError99=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Max(Math.Abs(origin.x),Math.Abs(origin.y)),Math.Max(Math.Abs(origin.x+width),Math.Abs(origin.y+width))))+1e-10;P lo99,hi99;t.ExpandedBounds99(maximumError99,out lo99,out hi99);int fx0=Math.Max(x0*2,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(x0*2,Math.Min(x1*2+2,(lo99.x-origin.x)*2)))),fx1=Math.Min(x1*2+1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(x0*2-1,Math.Min(x1*2+1,(hi99.x-origin.x)*2)))),fy0=Math.Max(y0*2,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(y0*2,Math.Min(y1*2+2,(lo99.y-origin.y)*2)))),fy1=Math.Min(y1*2+1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(y0*2-1,Math.Min(y1*2+1,(hi99.y-origin.y)*2))));
     for(int fy=fy0;fy<=fy1;fy++)for(int fx=fx0;fx<=fx1;fx++){if(++fineCount99>262144)throw new InvalidOperationException("99 bounded fine index budget before GPU submit");int at99=fy*width*2+fx;if(fineCells99[at99]==null)fineCells99[at99]=new List<Triangle>();fineCells99[at99].Add(t);}
    }
   }entries=count;fineEntries99=fineCount99;
  }
  public Probe Query(P p,double lineZ,P lineGradient){QueryScope103();
   if(++queries>131072)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 bounded CPU point query budget");int x=(int)Math.Floor(p.x-origin.x),y=(int)Math.Floor(p.y-origin.y);if(x<0||y<0||x>=width||y>=width)return new Probe(0,0);var bucket=cells[y*width+x];double error=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(p.x),Math.Abs(p.y)));double radius=Math.Min(Math.Min(p.x-origin.x-x,origin.x+x+1-p.x),Math.Min(p.y-origin.y-y,origin.y+y+1-p.y));bool unknown=false;
   if(bucket!=null)foreach(var t in bucket){if(++visits>16777216)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 CPU triangle visit budget before GPU submit");double u,v;t.Bary(p,out u,out v);double outside=t.Outside(p);if(outside>error){radius=Math.Min(radius,outside-error);continue;}
    double gap=t.Z(p)-lineZ,zError=32*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(lineZ),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.zb),Math.Abs(t.zc)))))+error*(Math.Abs(t.gradient.x)+Math.Abs(t.gradient.y)+Math.Abs(lineGradient.x)+Math.Abs(lineGradient.y));
    if(gap>zError){double slope=(t.gradient-lineGradient).Length;radius=Math.Min(radius,slope==0?radius:(gap-zError)/slope);continue;}
    if(outside>=-error||u<0||v<0||u+v>1){unknown=true;continue;}
    int alpha=t.Alpha(p,error);if(alpha<0)continue;if(alpha==0||gap>=-zError){unknown=true;continue;}return new Probe(-1,0);
   }
   return unknown?new Probe(0,0):new Probe(1,Math.Max(0,radius));
  }
  public Probe QueryVisible99(P p,double lineZ,P lineGradient){QueryScope103();
   if(++queries>131072)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 bounded CPU point query budget");int x=(int)Math.Floor(p.x-origin.x),y=(int)Math.Floor(p.y-origin.y);if(x<0||y<0||x>=width||y>=width)return new Probe(0,0);int fx99=(int)Math.Floor((p.x-origin.x)*2),fy99=(int)Math.Floor((p.y-origin.y)*2);var bucket=fineCells99[fy99*width*2+fx99];double error=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(p.x),Math.Abs(p.y)));bool unknown=false;
   if(bucket!=null)foreach(var t in bucket){if(++visits>16777216)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 CPU triangle visit budget before GPU submit");double gap=t.Z(p)-lineZ,zError=32*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(lineZ),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.zb),Math.Abs(t.zc)))))+error*(Math.Abs(t.gradient.x)+Math.Abs(t.gradient.y)+Math.Abs(lineGradient.x)+Math.Abs(lineGradient.y));
    if(gap>zError)continue;double outside=t.Outside(p);if(outside>error)continue;double u,v;t.Bary(p,out u,out v);
    if(outside>=-error||u<0||v<0||u+v>1){unknown=true;continue;}
    int alpha=t.Alpha(p,error);if(alpha<0)continue;if(alpha==0||gap>=-zError){unknown=true;continue;}return new Probe(-1,0);
   }
   return unknown?new Probe(0,0):new Probe(1,0);
  }
  public bool ClearSegment(P a,P b,Triangle strip,P shift){QueryScope103();P aa=a-shift,bb=b-shift;double error=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Max(Math.Abs(aa.x),Math.Abs(aa.y)),Math.Max(Math.Abs(bb.x),Math.Abs(bb.y))))+strip.xyError;int x0=(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(aa.x,bb.x)-error-origin.x),x1=(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(aa.x,bb.x)+error-origin.x),y0=(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(aa.y,bb.y)-error-origin.y),y1=(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(aa.y,bb.y)+error-origin.y);if(x0<0||y0<0||x1>=width||y1>=width)return false;segmentCandidates.Clear();for(int y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(int x=x0;x<=x1;x++){var bucket=cells[y*width+x];if(bucket!=null)foreach(var t in bucket)segmentCandidates.Add(t);}
   foreach(var t in segmentCandidates){if(++visits>16777216)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 CPU triangle visit budget before GPU submit");double lo,hi;if(!t.SegmentInterval(aa,bb,error,out lo,out hi))continue;
    double za=t.Z(aa)-strip.Z(a),zb=t.Z(bb)-strip.Z(b),zError=32*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(strip.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.zb),Math.Abs(t.zc)))))+error*(t.gradient.Length+strip.gradient.Length);if(za>zError&&zb>zError)continue;if((za>zError)!=(zb>zError)){double cut=(zError-za)/(zb-za);if(za>zError)lo=Math.Max(lo,cut);else hi=Math.Min(hi,cut);}if(lo>hi)continue;
    P middle=aa+(bb-aa)*((lo+hi)*.5);double radius=(bb-aa).Length*(hi-lo)*.5+error;if(t.Alpha(middle,radius)!=-1)return false;
   }return true;
  }
  public bool Covered(List<P> polygon,Triangle strip,P shift){QueryScope103();P centre=new P();foreach(P p in polygon)centre+=p;centre=centre*(1.0/polygon.Count);P q=centre-shift;int x=(int)Math.Floor(q.x-origin.x),y=(int)Math.Floor(q.y-origin.y);if(x<0||y<0||x>=width||y>=width)return false;int fx99=(int)Math.Floor((q.x-origin.x)*2),fy99=(int)Math.Floor((q.y-origin.y)*2);var bucket=fineCells99[fy99*width*2+fx99];if(bucket==null)return false;double error=8*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(q.x),Math.Abs(q.y)))+strip.xyError,radius=error;foreach(P p in polygon)radius=Math.Max(radius,(p-centre).Length+error);
   foreach(var t in bucket){if(++visits>16777216)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 CPU triangle visit budget before GPU submit");bool covers=true;double zError=32*FloatEpsilon*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(strip.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.za),Math.Max(Math.Abs(t.zb),Math.Abs(t.zc)))))+error*(t.gradient.Length+strip.gradient.Length);
    foreach(P point in polygon){P at=point-shift;if(t.Z(at)>=strip.Z(point)-zError||t.Outside(at)>=-error){covers=false;break;}}
    if(covers&&t.Alpha(q,radius)==1)return true;
   }return false;
  }
 }
 public sealed class Pixel {
  public bool support,unknown,visible,allCovered=true;public P witness,canonical,gradient;public double z,sourceUpper,radius,best=double.PositiveInfinity,t;public int edge;
  public int State{get{return !support?0:visible?1:allCovered?2:3;}} // Empty only when every source piece has a coverage proof.
 }
 public sealed class Raster {
  public readonly Pixel[] pixels;public readonly P origin,shift;public readonly int width;public int fragments;readonly Scene scene;readonly int firstRow103,lastRow103;public FragmentVisitor97 fragmentVisitor;
  public Raster(Scene scene,P origin,int width,P shift){this.scene=scene;this.origin=origin;this.width=width;this.shift=shift;firstRow103=0;lastRow103=width;pixels=new Pixel[width*width];for(int i=0;i<pixels.Length;i++)pixels[i]=new Pixel();}
  public Raster(Scene scene,Raster shared,int firstRow,int lastRow){if(firstRow<0||lastRow>shared.width||firstRow>=lastRow)throw new ArgumentException("103 disjoint row interval");this.scene=scene;origin=shared.origin;width=shared.width;shift=shared.shift;pixels=shared.pixels;firstRow103=firstRow;lastRow103=lastRow;}
  public static double Area(List<P> p){if(p.Count<3)return 0;double sum=0;P first=p[0];for(int i=1;i<p.Count-1;i++)sum+=P.Cross(p[i]-first,p[i+1]-first);return Math.Abs(sum)*.5;}
  public static List<P> Half(List<P> p,P n,double c){var o=new List<P>();if(p.Count==0)return o;P old=p[p.Count-1];double a=P.Dot(old,n)+c;foreach(P q in p){double b=P.Dot(q,n)+c;if((a<=0)!=(b<=0))o.Add(old+(q-old)*(a/(a-b)));if(b<=0)o.Add(q);old=q;a=b;}return o;}
  public void FinishClearance99(){foreach(var pixel in pixels){if(!pixel.visible)continue;var source=scene.Query(pixel.witness-shift,pixel.z,pixel.gradient);var destination=scene.Query(pixel.witness,pixel.z,pixel.gradient);if(source.state!=1||destination.state!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("99 visibility and diagnostic-clearance invariant");pixel.radius=Math.Min(source.clearance,destination.clearance);}}
  public void Add(Triangle strip,int edge,double ta,double tb,double tc){if(strip.det==0)return;int x0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(strip.a.x,Math.Min(strip.b.x,strip.c.x))-origin.x)),x1=Math.Min(width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(strip.a.x,Math.Max(strip.b.x,strip.c.x))-origin.x)),y0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(strip.a.y,Math.Min(strip.b.y,strip.c.y))-origin.y)),y1=Math.Min(width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(strip.a.y,Math.Max(strip.b.y,strip.c.y))-origin.y));
   y0=Math.Max(y0,firstRow103);y1=Math.Min(y1,lastRow103-1);for(int y=y0;y<=y1;y++)for(int x=x0;x<=x1;x++){P lo=origin+new P(x,y),hi=lo+new P(1,1);var p=new List<P>{strip.a,strip.b,strip.c};p=Half(p,new P(-1,0),lo.x);p=Half(p,new P(1,0),-hi.x);p=Half(p,new P(0,-1),lo.y);p=Half(p,new P(0,1),-hi.y);double area=Area(p);if(area<=0)continue;if(++fragments>8192)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 clipped fragment budget");var result=pixels[y*width+x];result.support=true;result.sourceUpper=Math.Min(1,result.sourceUpper+area);bool covered=scene.Covered(p,strip,shift)||scene.Covered(p,strip,new P());result.allCovered&=covered;if(covered){if(fragmentVisitor!=null)fragmentVisitor(strip,edge,p,true,x,y,false,new P());continue;}bool fragmentVisible=false;P fragmentWitness=new P();double fragmentDistance=double.PositiveInfinity;P centre=new P();foreach(P q in p)centre+=q;centre=centre*(1.0/p.Count);var trials=new List<P>{centre};foreach(P q in p)trials.Add((q+centre)*.5);
    foreach(P point in trials){double z=strip.Z(point);Probe source=scene.QueryVisible99(point-shift,z,strip.gradient);if(source.state!=1){result.unknown=true;continue;}Probe destination=scene.QueryVisible99(point,z,strip.gradient);if(destination.state!=1){result.unknown=true;continue;}double radius=Math.Min(source.clearance,destination.clearance),distance=P.Dot(point-(lo+new P(.5,.5)),point-(lo+new P(.5,.5))),roundoff=64*2.220446049250313e-16*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(point.x),Math.Abs(point.y)))*(1+distance);if(distance<fragmentDistance){fragmentVisible=true;fragmentDistance=distance;fragmentWitness=point;}if(!result.visible||distance<result.best-roundoff||Math.Abs(distance-result.best)<=roundoff&&edge<result.edge){double u,v;strip.Bary(point,out u,out v);result.visible=true;result.best=distance;result.witness=point;result.canonical=point-shift;result.gradient=strip.gradient;result.z=z;result.edge=edge;result.t=(1-u-v)*ta+u*tb+v*tc;result.radius=radius;}}if(fragmentVisitor!=null)fragmentVisitor(strip,edge,p,false,x,y,fragmentVisible,fragmentWitness);
   }
  }
 }

 public void Require(string t,string id){if(disposed||t!=topology||id!=eye)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 model/view cache invalidated");for(int r=0;r<source.Length;r++)for(int s=0;s<source[r].Length;s++)if(!source[r][s]||source[r][s].ComputeCRC()!=crc[r][s])throw new InvalidOperationException("97 original material changed; invalidate before submit");for(int i=0;i<textures.Count;i++)if(!textures[i]||textures[i].updateCount!=updates[i]||textures[i].imageContentsHash.ToString()!=hashes[i])throw new InvalidOperationException("97 original texture changed; invalidate before submit");}
 public LineGeometry97.Scene Prepare(UnityEngine.Mesh[] mesh,Matrix4x4[] matrix,Func<Vector3,Vector2> project,Matrix4x4 view,P97 origin,int width){int at=0;double start99=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup,tick99=start99,read99=0,project99=0,triangle99=0;
  for(int r=0;r<mesh.Length;r++){tick99=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;var v=mesh[r].vertices;var uv=mesh[r].uv;if(v.Length!=uv.Length)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 original UV missing");read99+=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-tick99;tick99=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;var p=new P97[v.Length];var z=new double[v.Length];for(int i=0;i<v.Length;i++){var world=matrix[r].MultiplyPoint3x4(v[i]);var q=project(world);if(!float.IsFinite(q.x)||!float.IsFinite(q.y))throw new InvalidOperationException("97 nonfinite current projection");p[i]=new P97(q.x,272-q.y);z[i]=-((double)view.m20*world.x+(double)view.m21*world.y+(double)view.m22*world.z+view.m23);}
   project99+=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-tick99;tick99=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;for(int s=0;s<mesh[r].subMeshCount;s++){var indices=mesh[r].GetIndices(s);for(int i=0;i<indices.Length;i+=3){int a=indices[i],b=indices[i+1],c=indices[i+2];var ua=new P97(uv[a].x,uv[a].y);var ub=new P97(uv[b].x,uv[b].y);var uc=new P97(uv[c].x,uv[c].y);if(at>=65536)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 actor triangle budget");if(at==triangles.Count)triangles.Add(new Triangle97(p[a],p[b],p[c],z[a],z[b],z[c],ua,ub,uc,materials[r][s]));else{if(triangles[at].material!=materials[r][s])throw new InvalidOperationException("97 source topology/material changed");triangles[at].Update(p[a],p[b],p[c],z[a],z[b],z[c],ua,ub,uc);}at++;}}triangle99+=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-tick99;
  }tick99=EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup;if(at!=triangles.Count)throw new InvalidOperationException("97 source topology count changed");if(scene==null)scene=new LineGeometry97.Scene(triangles,origin-new P97(1,1),width+2);else scene.Rebuild(triangles,origin-new P97(1,1));profile99=new Obj40{["readMs"]=1000*read99,["projectMs"]=1000*project99,["triangleMs"]=1000*triangle99,["indexMs"]=1000*(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-tick99),["totalMs"]=1000*(EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup-start99)};return scene;

namespace LineGeometry97 {
 public sealed class CurveOpacity98 {
  public sealed class Witness {public P point,gradient;public double z,t;public int edge;public Triangle strip;}
  sealed class Feature {public P a,b;public double width;}
  sealed class Part {public Triangle strip;public int edge;public bool known;public P witness;}
  readonly Scene scene;readonly Raster raster;readonly Dictionary<int,Feature> features=new Dictionary<int,Feature>();readonly List<Part>[] parts;readonly Dictionary<Triangle,Witness>[] curve;public readonly int[] masks;public readonly Witness[] selected;public readonly double[] maximumCurveDepth;public int curveWitnesses,continuityChecks;public Func<int,Scene> QueryForRow103;bool featuresSealed103;
  public void SealFeatures103(){featuresSealed103=true;}
  public CurveOpacity98(Scene scene,Raster raster){this.scene=scene;this.raster=raster;int n=raster.pixels.Length;parts=new List<Part>[n];curve=new Dictionary<Triangle,Witness>[n];masks=new int[n];selected=new Witness[n];maximumCurveDepth=new double[n];raster.fragmentVisitor=Visit;}
  public void Register(int edge,P a,P b,double width){if(featuresSealed103)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 immutable feature table during row work");if(features.Count>=1024)throw new InvalidOperationException("98 source feature budget");features.Add(edge,new Feature{a=a,b=b,width=width});}
  static bool Segment(List<P> polygon,P a,P b,double sign,out double lo,out double hi){lo=0;hi=1;for(int i=0;i<polygon.Count;i++){P start=polygon[i],e=polygon[(i+1)%polygon.Count]-start;double x=sign*P.Cross(e,a-start),y=sign*P.Cross(e,b-start);if(x<0&&y<0)return false;if((x<0)!=(y<0)){double cut=x/(x-y);if(x<0)lo=Math.Max(lo,cut);else hi=Math.Min(hi,cut);}}return lo<hi;}
  public void Visit(Triangle strip,int edge,List<P> polygon,bool covered,int x,int y,bool visible,P witness){Scene query103=QueryForRow103==null?scene:QueryForRow103(y);if(covered)return;int at=y*raster.width+x;if(parts[at]==null)parts[at]=new List<Part>();parts[at].Add(new Part{strip=strip,edge=edge,known=visible,witness=witness});Feature f=features[edge];double lo,hi;if(!Segment(polygon,f.a,f.b,strip.det>0?1:-1,out lo,out hi))return;
   foreach(double fraction in new[]{.5,.25,.75}){P p=f.a+(f.b-f.a)*(lo+(hi-lo)*fraction);double epsilon=64*2.220446049250313e-16*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(p.x),Math.Abs(p.y)));P relative=p-(raster.origin+new P(x,y));if(relative.x<=epsilon||relative.y<=epsilon||relative.x>=1-epsilon||relative.y>=1-epsilon)continue;p=new P((float)p.x,(float)p.y);double z=(float)strip.Z(p);if(query103.QueryVisible99(p-raster.shift,z,strip.gradient).state!=1||query103.QueryVisible99(p,z,strip.gradient).state!=1)continue;double u,v;strip.Bary(p,out u,out v);double t=P.Dot(p-f.a,f.b-f.a)/P.Dot(f.b-f.a,f.b-f.a);var w=new Witness{point=p,z=z,gradient=strip.gradient,t=t,edge=edge,strip=strip};if(curve[at]==null)curve[at]=new Dictionary<Triangle,Witness>();Witness prior;
    if(!curve[at].TryGetValue(strip,out prior)||Distance(w,x,y)<Distance(prior,x,y)||Distance(w,x,y)==Distance(prior,x,y)&&w.t<prior.t)curve[at][strip]=w;
   }
  }
  double Distance(Witness p,int x,int y){P d=p.point-(raster.origin+new P(x+.5,y+.5));return P.Dot(d,d);}
  public void Finish(float[] currentStateProof){int width=raster.width;
   for(int at=0;at<parts.Length;at++){int x=at%width,y=at/width;if(currentStateProof[(y*width*2+x)*4]!=1||curve[at]==null){curve[at]=null;continue;}foreach(var w in curve[at].Values){curveWitnesses++;maximumCurveDepth[at]=Math.Max(maximumCurveDepth[at],w.z);Witness old=selected[at];double a=Distance(w,x,y),b=old==null?double.PositiveInfinity:Distance(old,x,y),error=64*2.220446049250313e-16*(1+Math.Max(Math.Abs(w.point.x),Math.Abs(w.point.y)))*(1+a);if(old==null||a<b-error||Math.Abs(a-b)<=error&&(w.edge<old.edge||w.edge==old.edge&&w.t<old.t))selected[at]=w;}}
   for(int at=0;at<parts.Length;at++){int x=at%width,y=at/width;if(currentStateProof[(y*width*2+x)*4]!=1||selected[at]!=null||parts[at]==null)continue;int required=511^16;bool all=true;
    foreach(var part in parts[at]){if(!part.known||features[part.edge].width+2*part.strip.xyError>=1){all=false;break;}int suitable=0;
     for(int dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(int dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){if(dx==0&&dy==0)continue;int nx=x+dx,ny=y+dy;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=width||ny>=width)continue;int target=ny*width+nx;Witness w;if(curve[target]==null||!curve[target].TryGetValue(part.strip,out w))continue;continuityChecks++;if(scene.ClearSegment(part.witness,w.point,part.strip,raster.shift)&&scene.ClearSegment(part.witness,w.point,part.strip,new P()))suitable|=1<<((dy+1)*3+dx+1);}
     if(suitable==0){all=false;break;}required&=suitable;
    }if(all)masks[at]=required;
   }
  }
 }
}

public sealed class SourceWidthCache99:IDisposable {
 public sealed class Entry {
  public readonly Texture2D texture;readonly int width,height;readonly uint update;readonly string imageHash;readonly Dictionary<Vector2,float> values=new Dictionary<Vector2,float>();readonly SourceWidthCache99 owner;
  public Entry(Texture2D t,SourceWidthCache99 owner){texture=t;this.owner=owner;width=t.width;height=t.height;update=t.updateCount;imageHash=t.imageContentsHash.ToString();Require();}
  public void Require(){var t=texture;if(!t||t.width!=width||t.height!=height||t.updateCount!=update||t.imageContentsHash.ToString()!=imageHash||!t.isReadable||t.mipmapCount!=1||t.filterMode!=FilterMode.Bilinear||t.wrapModeU!=TextureWrapMode.Repeat||t.wrapModeV!=TextureWrapMode.Repeat||GraphicsFormatUtility.IsSRGBFormat(t.graphicsFormat))throw new InvalidOperationException("99 source width texture/sampler changed; invalidate before submit");}
  public float Sample(float u,float v){if(owner.disposed||!float.IsFinite(u)||!float.IsFinite(v))throw new InvalidOperationException("99 width source lifetime/UV");var key=new Vector2(u,v);float value;if(values.TryGetValue(key,out value)){owner.hits++;return value;}if(++owner.entries>8192)throw new InvalidOperationException("99 source UV cache budget");double x=(double)u*width-.5,y=(double)v*height-.5;int x0=(int)Math.Floor(x),y0=(int)Math.Floor(y);double dx=x-x0,dy=y-y0;int Wrap(int i,int n){i%=n;return i<0?i+n:i;}double a=texture.GetPixel(Wrap(x0,width),Wrap(y0,height),0).g,b=texture.GetPixel(Wrap(x0+1,width),Wrap(y0,height),0).g,c=texture.GetPixel(Wrap(x0,width),Wrap(y0+1,height),0).g,d=texture.GetPixel(Wrap(x0+1,width),Wrap(y0+1,height),0).g;value=(float)(((1-dx)*a+dx*b)*(1-dy)+((1-dx)*c+dx*d)*dy);values.Add(key,value);owner.misses++;return value;}
  public void Clear(){values.Clear();}
 }
 readonly Dictionary<Texture2D,Entry> textures=new Dictionary<Texture2D,Entry>();bool disposed;int entries;public int hits,misses;public int Entries=>entries;
 public Entry Require(Texture2D t){if(disposed)throw new ObjectDisposedException("99 source width cache");Entry entry;if(textures.TryGetValue(t,out entry))entry.Require();else{if(textures.Count>=16)throw new InvalidOperationException("99 source width texture budget");entry=new Entry(t,this);textures.Add(t,entry);}return entry;}
 public void Dispose(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;foreach(var entry in textures.Values)entry.Clear();textures.Clear();entries=0;/* Borrowed source textures are never disposed or changed. */}
}

  public Strip103(Triangle triangle,int edge,double a,double b,double c){this.triangle=triangle;this.edge=edge;this.a=a;this.b=b;this.c=c;}
 }
 public sealed class RowStats103 {public int workers,splitRow;public long topCandidateCells,bottomCandidateCells;public double mainMs,workerMs,joinMs,totalMs;public bool allWorkersCompleted,queryViewsReleased;}
 public static class RowParallel103 {
  // TickCount is a public monotonic millisecond clock in the already supplied
  // mscorlib. Unsigned subtraction handles its wrap; outer high-resolution
  // Editor timing still measures the entire preparation phase on its owner.
  static double Elapsed103(int start){return unchecked((uint)(Environment.TickCount-start));}
  public static int Split103(Raster raster,List<Strip103> strips,out long top,out long bottom){
   // Partition only scheduling, using the exact candidate-cell bounds of Add.
   // No source shape/colour/visibility or output selection is changed.
   var rows=new long[raster.width];long total=0;
   foreach(var s in strips){var t=s.triangle;if(t.det==0)continue;
    int x0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(t.a.x,Math.Min(t.b.x,t.c.x))-raster.origin.x)),x1=Math.Min(raster.width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(t.a.x,Math.Max(t.b.x,t.c.x))-raster.origin.x)),y0=Math.Max(0,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Min(t.a.y,Math.Min(t.b.y,t.c.y))-raster.origin.y)),y1=Math.Min(raster.width-1,(int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(t.a.y,Math.Max(t.b.y,t.c.y))-raster.origin.y));
    if(x0>x1)continue;for(int y=y0;y<=y1;y++){rows[y]+=x1-x0+1;total+=x1-x0+1;}
   }
   int split=1;long prefix=0,best=long.MaxValue;top=0;bottom=total;
   for(int y=1;y<raster.width;y++){prefix+=rows[y-1];long cost=Math.Max(prefix,total-prefix);if(cost<best){best=cost;split=y;top=prefix;bottom=total-prefix;}}
   return split;
  }
  // Scene geometry/index, source images and feature registration are immutable
  // until both row partitions complete. No Unity API or GPU resource is used
  // by the worker. Each pixel and its feature dictionaries have one writer.
  public static RowStats103 Run(Scene scene,Raster raster,CurveOpacity98 collector,List<Strip103> strips){
   if(strips==null||strips.Count>2048)throw new ArgumentException("103 bounded source strips");int clock=Environment.TickCount;var result=new RowStats103();collector.SealFeatures103();
   if(raster.width<2||Environment.ProcessorCount<2){foreach(var s in strips)raster.Add(s.triangle,s.edge,s.a,s.b,s.c);result.workers=1;result.allWorkersCompleted=true;result.queryViewsReleased=scene.ActiveQueryViews103==0;result.totalMs=Elapsed103(clock);return result;}
   Scene top=null,bottom=null;Raster first=null,second=null;System.Threading.Tasks.Task worker=null;var errors=new List<Exception>();int split=Split103(raster,strips,out result.topCandidateCells,out result.bottomCandidateCells);result.splitRow=split;double workerMs=0;result.workers=2;
   try{
    top=scene.ForkQueries103();bottom=scene.ForkQueries103();first=new Raster(top,raster,0,split);second=new Raster(bottom,raster,split,raster.width);first.fragmentVisitor=collector.Visit;second.fragmentVisitor=collector.Visit;Scene firstQuery=top,secondQuery=bottom;collector.QueryForRow103=y=>y<split?firstQuery:secondQuery;
    var immutable=strips.ToArray();Raster firstRaster=first;worker=System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Factory.StartNew(()=>{int watch=Environment.TickCount;try{foreach(var s in immutable)firstRaster.Add(s.triangle,s.edge,s.a,s.b,s.c);}finally{workerMs=Elapsed103(watch);}},System.Threading.CancellationToken.None,System.Threading.Tasks.TaskCreationOptions.None,System.Threading.Tasks.TaskScheduler.Default);
    int mainClock=Environment.TickCount;try{foreach(var s in immutable)second.Add(s.triangle,s.edge,s.a,s.b,s.c);}catch(Exception e){errors.Add(e);}finally{result.mainMs=Elapsed103(mainClock);}
   }catch(Exception e){errors.Add(e);}
   finally{
    // Always join, including main-partition failure. A read lease is never
    // released by a timeout while a task might still be using its source.
    int join=Environment.TickCount;if(worker!=null)try{worker.Wait();}catch(Exception e){errors.Add(e);}result.joinMs=Elapsed103(join);result.workerMs=workerMs;result.allWorkersCompleted=worker==null||worker.IsCompleted;collector.QueryForRow103=null;if(top!=null)top.ReleaseQueries103();if(bottom!=null)bottom.ReleaseQueries103();result.queryViewsReleased=scene.ActiveQueryViews103==0;
   }
   if(errors.Count!=0)throw new AggregateException("103 CPU row work failed after all tasks joined",errors);
   scene.queries+=top.queries+bottom.queries;scene.visits+=top.visits+bottom.visits;raster.fragments=first.fragments+second.fragments;if(scene.queries>131072||scene.visits>16777216||raster.fragments>8192)throw new InvalidOperationException("103 aggregate CPU budget before GPU submission");result.totalMs=Elapsed103(clock);return result;
  }
 }
}
